# Windvale Seed verification evidence

- Evidence date: 2026-08-04
- Milestone: Windvale Seed
- Qualified hosts: Windows x64 and Debian Linux x64
- Latest cross-host Seed qualified implementation commit: `524e84afb6e5bab6bbd95ebc0b9eeaf886af834b`
- Latest pinned Windvale OS qualified implementation commit: `524e84afb6e5bab6bbd95ebc0b9eeaf886af834b`

## Requirement evidence

| Requirement | Evidence |
| --- | --- |
| C# Stage 0 toolchain | Pinned .NET SDK, solution, compiler, bytecode, runtime, object-model oracle, WVA assembler, linker oracle, CLI, and test projects build with warnings treated as errors. |
| Source-to-bytecode path | `Sum-Data.wv`, `Hello-Windvale.wv`, `Read-Wvb-Header.wv`, `Wv-Dump-Core.wv`, `Wvo-Object-Core.wv`, `Wva-Assembler-Core.wv`, and `Wv-Linker-Core.wv` compile through syntax, semantic WIR, lowering, canonical encoding, and mandatory verification. |
| Deterministic portable modules | Repeated compilation and codec round trips compare complete bytes; fixed SHA-256 identities are checked for every qualified Windvale example and composed tool artifact. |
| Module, assembly, object, and link CLI | The native verifiers invoke `compile`, `assemble`, `link`, `inspect`, `verify`, `run`, `object-inspect`, and `object-verify` against generated artifacts. |
| Reference runtime | Portable .NET interpreter executes only `Verifiedˉmodule`, with checked signed and unsigned arithmetic, array and byte-range bounds, step, call-depth, capability, and host-result controls. |
| Explicit capabilities | Hosted arguments, file-byte input and output, standard output, line output, and diagnostics must be declared, supported by the selected host, and granted separately; refusal and success are tested. |
| Hosted resource boundary | Arguments are immutable and UTF-8 bounded, native file reads stream into bounded immutable bytes, each exact resource name has one first-successful-read snapshot per context, at most 64 distinct snapshots are retained, file writes replace one bounded whole value and durably flush, normal and diagnostic sinks are separate, and stable missing/invalid/oversized/bad-host/snapshot-limit cases are exercised. |
| Useful diagnostics | Language compiler, assembly parser/semantics, binary readers, link resolution/layout/relocation, verifiers, and runtime failures expose stable codes; source errors retain one-based line and column. |
| Source conventions | U+02C9 identifiers and exported `Main` compile and execute; immutable `let` locals and parameters reject assignment; mutable `var` locals accept it; malformed and confusable separators are rejected. |
| Bounded static source modules | Explicit `import` graphs are resolved only from supplied strict-UTF-8 sources, bounded to 64 modules and 16 MiB of aggregate characters, rejected when missing, duplicate, cyclic, unreferenced, non-portable, or outside the first function-only shape, and composed deterministically regardless of dependency argument order. Dependencies are semantically isolated from root and sibling declarations, dependency functions are internalized, and only root exports remain WVB exports. |
| First two-consumer Foundation module | `Foundationˉmachineˉcontracts` owns exact bounded WVO alignment and whole-value machine-name predicates, is imported by both the Windvale assembler and linker, and preserves diagnostic ownership plus every canonical object, image, map, and failure boundary. |
| Shared ordinal byte-span ordering | `Foundationˉbyteˉordering` owns unsigned lexicographic comparison for validated spans of one immutable byte value and is consumed by the Windvale WVO object core, assembler, and linker without allocation, text decoding, or raised instruction ceilings. |
| Foundation binary and text primitives | `u8`, `u32`, immutable `bytes`, zero-copy slices, immutable concatenation, signed and unsigned little-endian reads and fixed-width construction, exact SHA-256 identity over sequences and slices, explicit `u8` to `u32` and `u32` to `u64` widening, strict UTF-8 validation/encoding/decoding, and ASCII-safe quoting are type-checked, verified, inspected, executed, and covered by deterministic trap and size-limit tests. |
| Windvale-written compiler lexer | `Compilerˉsourceˉlexer` streams the complete implemented Seed keyword/operator surface over strict UTF-8 bytes, preserves retained Stage 0 token identities and UTF-16-compatible positions, classifies bounded integer literals, validates string escapes and surrogate pairs, and reports deterministic bounded failures without allocating a token collection. The current candidate includes constants, match/variant/collection keywords, loop control, Boolean/compound operators, division/remainder, bitwise operators, and shifts. |
| Windvale-written compiler parser | Qualified declaration and body passes reproduce the complete implemented top-level, statement, and expression grammar as bounded immutable source views while retaining no token, declaration, statement, expression, or syntax-tree collection. The current candidate includes typed constants, metadata, nominal variants, named records, `else if`, `match`, bounded sequences/builders, `for`, loop control, short-circuit Boolean expressions, compound assignment, and the extended operator precedence table. |
| Canonical compiler source set | WVSS 1 carries one root and as many as 63 canonically ordered dependencies in one validated immutable envelope. The portable Windvale module enforces structure, every qualified frontend pass, unique names, and dependency profile/shape rules without admitting paths, timestamps, host objects, or host collection ordering. |
| Windvale-written compiler import graph | `Compilerˉsourceˉgraph` resolves exact declared module-name spans, rejects duplicate and missing imports, computes the complete root closure, rejects unreachable supplied modules, and proves acyclicity with deterministic bounded traversal over one immutable state byte per module. |
| Windvale-written declaration/signature binding | `Compilerˉsourceˉsymbols` enforces namespaces and capability policy, resolves visible nominal signature types, validates root typed constants, assigns canonical nominal identities, and publishes independently validated `WVSD 1.1` plus transitive-visibility evidence. |
| Windvale-written body binding | `Compilerˉsourceˉbindings` assigns canonical parameter/local slots and lexical scopes, binds reads including storage-free constants, assignments, constructors, functions, capabilities, and Foundation intrinsics, checks arity, and publishes independently validated `WVLB 1.1` evidence. Its parameter and finalization phases let typed lowering construct the same local evidence without a second successful-path body traversal. |
| Canonical typed source IR | `Compilerˉsourceˉwir` performs complete implemented expression typing, field/operator/call validation, explicit basic-block and temporary construction, return/reachability proof, and publication only after independent validation of all five packed `WVIR 1` sections and their semantic relationships. It carries function-private binding state through the same statement traversal and preserves independent binding diagnostics on failure. The current candidate adds structurally verified Boolean phi, variant/match, bounded collection/builder, `for`, nearest-loop target, compound-assignment, and complete operator evidence. |
| Windvale-written WVB backend | `Compilerˉsourceˉwvb` resolves global WVSD identities through their owning WVSS source and statically flattens the complete validated graph into one canonical WVB 1.11 module. It preserves module metadata, profiles, capabilities, data, and exports; internalizes dependency functions and nominal types; canonically remaps every function/data/type/capability identity; lowers the complete implemented primitive, nominal, variant, collection, capability, Foundation, and operator surface including `i64`/`u64`; computes exact branch offsets and maximum stack in a separate pass; and rejects invalid graphs before publication. |
| Reproducible compiler bootstrap | The exact 12-module, 677,073-source-byte inventory is compiled by Stage 0 into a verified 599,868-byte Stage 1 Windvale compiler. Stage 1 compiles that same explicit inventory in 6,700,562,174 verified VM instructions into a separately verified, byte-identical Stage 2 compiler with SHA-256 `9673bf3331763181f443ec67b7a513bc66daa718969f7f6b0d197a4186071066`. Dedicated Windows and Linux verifiers fail on build, compile, runtime, publication, verification, or byte-identity differences. |
| Shared native WVB execution slices | Cross-host-qualified portable modules lower into typed locals, typed numbered values, canonical blocks, explicit terminators, explicit instruction charges, and independently decoded x86-64 fragments. Constants, checked arithmetic, control flow, calls through the prior four-register convention, shared instruction/depth budgets, immutable data, borrowed and arena-backed values, twelve native services, W^X JIT, and WVO-linked AOT agree on Windows and Debian x64. Qualified ABI 16 retains the first four registers and independently verifies stack cells through the language's 64-parameter bound; maximum-width scalar, descriptor, and void calls pass focused differential coverage. Unsupported WVB or hostile fragment bytes fail with stable diagnostics and no fallback. |
| First shared native WVB in Windvale OS | One ordinary portable module compiles to canonical verified WVB and the same ABI-5 WVO backend, then executes calls, a bounded loop, and immutable data on the kernel-owned OS stack. Exact result 29 gates continued version-7 boot under pinned QEMU; the guest does not yet load or verify WVB. |
| Immutable nominal records | Record declarations, positional construction, named field reads, nominal function signatures, canonical WVB schemas, verifier rejection cases, runtime values, and inspector output are exercised end to end. |
| Nominal enums and bounded formatting | Explicit enum declarations, exact nominal equality, enum-valued record fields, member naming, invariant `i32`/`u8`/`u32` formatting, bounded text concatenation, verifier rejection cases, and deterministic runtime output are exercised end to end. |
| Windvale-written inspection | `Wvˉdumpˉcore` reads an explicit real file through hosted resources while pure Windvale functions validate all seven envelopes, decode every declaration payload and value shape, walk every instruction, reject malformed lengths/UTF-8/opcodes without escaping diagnostic boundaries, and emit a versioned ASCII-safe line report. |
| WVO 1.0 object model | The C# oracle canonically encodes and strictly verifies x86-64-first sections, symbols, imports/exports, ranges, machine names, zero relocation placeholders, and non-overlapping `absolute-u32`/`relative-i32` relocations. |
| Windvale-written object verification and inspection | `Wvoˉobjectˉcore` constructs and structurally validates a 189-byte representative object in its internal self-test, then completely verifies and reports bounded existing WVO input through read-only authority. Successful reports match the C# oracle during native-candidate qualification. |
| WVA 1 Stage 0 assembler | The bounded parser enforces canonical declarations, definition ownership, contexts, integer widths, and references; the x86-64 encoder covers the complete initial code/data subset, derives ranges, creates both relocation kinds, and returns only independently verified WVO bytes. |
| Windvale-written WVA assembler | `Wvaˉassemblerˉcore` validates source size, strict UTF-8, physical-line bytes, line endings, token boundaries, the complete grammar, declarations, definitions, integer widths, contexts, limits, ownership, and references over immutable bytes without host text parsing. It measures the complete result, derives definition ranges, encodes every WVA 1 instruction/data statement, and constructs canonical WVO sections, symbols, and relocations. |
| Windvale linker object boundary | `Wvˉlinkerˉcore` validates the complete WVO 1.0 header, limits, names, sections, symbols, relocations, canonical order, definition ownership, placeholder zeros, and exact consumption in verified bytecode, then exposes immutable offset-based views for later link passes. Representative objects, 128 deterministic one-byte mutations, and 128 bounded random byte values are differentially classified against the independent C# WVO oracle. |
| Windvale linker resolution and layout | The same verified module enforces link-wide aggregate limits, merge-checks canonical export ranges, resolves every import with exact kind matching, selects an exported-function entry, places sections by kind/input/source order using actual-address alignment, bounds the image and `u32` address space, and recomputes every defined-symbol address. |
| Windvale linker image and relocation | The module constructs exact padding, materialized data, and zero-fill as one immutable persistent byte value; resolves local/export/import relocation targets; checks full `absolute-u32` and `relative-i32` arithmetic without truncation; patches exactly four bytes immutably; and emits the exact Stage 0 image digest. |
| Windvale linker independent reconstruction | A separately structured path recomputes placement, rescans all exports, derives symbol addresses independently, applies relocations in reverse traversal order with separate arithmetic, and compares the complete reconstructed image byte by byte. A mismatch returns `WVL1011`, clears candidate bytes, and leaves the writer unreachable. |
| Windvale linker canonical map and publication | After independent image agreement, the Windvale linker constructs the complete path-free ASCII/LF map with exact byte accounting and a 1 MiB limit, publishes the image with exactly one `file.write_bytes` call, and emits that map through standard output. Deterministic failures cannot reach the writer and preserve an existing output; host write failures remain a separate runtime boundary. |
| Stage 0 linker oracle | Windvale Linking 1 validates complete WVO inputs and aggregate limits, keeps locals object-private, resolves every import to one same-kind export, requires an exported-function entry, lays out actual addresses by section kind/input/source order, materializes zero padding and BSS, applies both relocation kinds with checked arithmetic, and independently reconstructs the complete image before returning bytes. |
| Canonical link evidence | The path-free ASCII/LF map records input digests, section placements, definitions, import providers, patch and target addresses, relocation values, entry, and image digest in deterministic order. It is byte-limited, locale-independent, and compared as a complete value across hosts. |
| Representative programs | The portable sum returns `29`; the hosted example prints `Hello from Windvale`; the Foundation header returns `1`; both Foundation boundary demos return `0`; WvDump emits the complete golden Sum report; the WVO core self-tests its exact representative and inspects the canonical assembler object; the hosted Windvale assembler writes the 218-byte canonical `Hello-Object.wvo`; and both the Windvale linker and Stage 0 link it with the 91-byte `Console-Provider.wvo` into the same independently verified 24-byte image and 1,721-byte map. |
| Malformed-input coverage | Structured adversarial cases plus deterministic bounded random Windvale source, WVA source, module, object, Windvale WVO-scan, and link input exercise diagnostic and rejection boundaries. Link coverage includes invalid and oversized objects, invalid entry names, duplicate exports, undefined imports, kind mismatches, non-function entries, aggregate section limits, map limits, unaligned bases, all section kinds, BSS/padding zeros, image/u32 overflow, absolute and relative relocation overflow, 200 hostile objects, locale changes, semantic input reordering, and missing/unchanged output cases. |
| Cross-host coverage | The same 60-test suite passed on Windows and Debian Linux, including native interpreter/JIT/AOT differential execution through each host's W^X adapter. Their normalized contracts compare equal, all 62 current portable WVB, WVO, image, and map artifacts are byte-identical, and both hosts retain the exact qualified Stage 1/Stage 2 compiler identity and portable instruction count. |
| Scope control | The qualified compiler supports bounded static nominal source composition through independently validated typed `WVIR 1`, direct canonical flattening of one complete validated graph into a portable, hosted, or system WVB, and exact bytecode self-reproduction. It covers imports, dependency functions and nominal types, root primitive data/functions, immutable records, nominal enums, and declarations/calls from the complete seven-entry Seed capability catalog; four focused Foundation modules and the qualified Windvale and Stage 0 linkers remain available. The shared native backend covers one exported portable `Main() -> i32`, internal i32/bool functions with as many as four parameters, constants, checked i32 arithmetic, comparisons, Boolean operations, forward/backward control flow, loops, returns, immutable i32 array data, and dynamic positive instruction/depth maxima. Capabilities, general collections, text/bytes, mutable globals, aggregates, allocation/reclamation, a runtime module linker, package manager, executable-container adapter, broad native instruction coverage, native compiler execution, directly executable host program, Windvale-written VM/JIT, .NET retirement, functioning kernel runtime, driver, and complete OS implementation are not included. |

## Verified on the implementation host

```powershell
pwsh -NoProfile -File Tools/Verify/Verify-Seed.ps1 -Level Qualification
```

The verifier performs a Release build, runs the 60-test conformance suite, produces a Windows report, publishes the CLI as framework-dependent `linux-x64`, and exercises real source composition, project selection, Foundation/compiler module composition, the complete compiler frontend through typed WVIR and the qualified canonical function/data/nominal/capability WVB backend, shared native fragments, Windvale-owned runtime stencils, module, Windvale/Stage 0 assembly, Windvale WVO scan, complete Windvale and Stage 0 link, object, inspect, verify, and runtime CLI paths. It checks dependency-order independence, semantic and binary-evidence rejection, output preservation, capability refusal and authorized execution, deterministic file snapshots, opt-in executed-instruction reporting, SHA-256 sequence/slice behavior, byte-for-byte compiler and assembler equality, independent complete-image reconstruction, exact Windvale/Stage 0 image and map equality, maximum image and map-limit cases, publish-after-success behavior, no file for rejected source/link or a missing parent, and preservation of existing outputs when validation fails. The current WVB 1.6 Windows report SHA-256 is `c34a2199e548631323b2186dda0dcf8ffcb0a3a3c6eb7d53d9a405c314837a4b`.

The expensive compiler convergence proof remains separate from the ordinary suite:

```powershell
pwsh -NoProfile -File Tools/Verify/Verify-Bootstrap.ps1
```

It builds Stage 1 from the exact inventory with the reference/recovery compiler, runs Stage 1 to build Stage 2, independently verifies Stage 2, and requires complete byte equality. Linux uses `./Tools/Verify/Verify-Bootstrap.sh` with the same inventory and ceiling.

## WVB 1.6 linker-prerequisite qualification

Candidate commit `348c82a` was archived and transferred with matching SHA-256 `985410209aee1cc54753aecd12d8d73658b7f3ffa646a129a4f490bec520817c`, then verified in `/tmp/windvale-prerequisites-348c82a-20260730` on the isolated [E-Worker](https://eworker.ca) QA host. The host ran Debian GNU/Linux 12 x64 with .NET SDK `10.0.302`; the reports identify .NET `10.0.10`. The exact archive passed the Release build with zero warnings/errors, all 31 tests, and the complete native CLI verifier. The Debian report was retrieved with SHA-256 `cec2e640181eb7bf5bca91fd7890aecd93137c6f594ca6af0bac873e8c98d3c0`. The report comparator confirmed that the Windows and Debian normalized contracts match.

Both hosts produced WVB format `1.6` with these module identities:

- `Sum-Data.wvb`: `6f3a272d37dd8893995c7f85c236414ed2864bf59de2f3775c08afd426013f8c`.
- `Hello-Windvale.wvb`: `bcf6597a27384661d2796f1dd8ee6e24cce8e6c7cb84def3b7826a564acb7d54`.
- `Read-Wvb-Header.wvb`: `72ae31559bb3335b320328c26e70518b6a0f3e617d099d41b328b066bb3784c7`.
- `Wv-Dump-Core.wvb`: `38af93371f5ed737946092092c67f6c363b340c7b2a2e8d0588c05a3e94b730b`.
- `Wvo-Object-Core.wvb`: `76f5a414bdc8feab35cedb28ecfc56d0ed24b0abcfc3c5c128e4f71fd0e5232b`.
- `Wva-Assembler-Core.wvb`: `9fe0e79a4895281908df13b31f127dd9dd019282263da71874bbefb7d9d3cb3a`.

The directly retrieved Debian `Wv-Dump-Core.wvb` and `Wva-Assembler-Core.wvb` matched their Windows artifacts byte for byte. Every pre-existing WVO, WVA, image, and map identity remained unchanged, including the 24-byte linked image `0e02d447ec379e8bc8be373694d6ca14fdde0125550cbd34ee05b3ecc63ffe9a` and 1,721-byte map `31bc6a8e90d5f3049ae3e2eb0735a901923186d6a03ed40f22762b557b2ba5f4`. Qualification specifically exercised opcode `0x7D` through compiler lowering, verifier stack typing, runtime SHA-256 over a non-zero-offset slice, inspector output, and the Windvale-written WvDump decoder. It also proved same-name snapshot reuse without a second adapter call, snapshot sharing across capability hosts for one context, the 64-distinct-snapshot ceiling, and the 67-argument launcher ceiling. After evidence retrieval, the resolved exact QA directory and transferred archive were removed and confirmed absent.

This qualifies Decision 0012 and the prerequisite portion of roadmap gate 6G. It does not qualify the Windvale-written linker itself.

## Balanced persistent-byte qualification

Candidate commit `89ce80b` was archived with SHA-256 `a0b6620227b04315936e103a1e42e4d99c14649cc3de1e1e3b8fd30895619452` and verified from that exact archive in `/tmp/windvale-persistent-bytes-89ce80b-20260730` on the Debian QA host. Windows and Debian both completed a zero-warning Release build, all 32 tests, and the complete native CLI verifier. The added Windvale test performs 65,536 one-byte concatenations, validates both endpoints, creates and reads a four-byte patch across persistent slice/concat boundaries, and proves that the original sequence remains unchanged. Both reports retained SHA-256 `5711f3025ea56edcc3af017505604c3c4153ad38e5858dedfea73b0d121366e3` on Windows and `cec2e640181eb7bf5bca91fd7890aecd93137c6f594ca6af0bac873e8c98d3c0` on Debian because the normalized portable contract and every canonical artifact stayed unchanged. The comparator confirmed equality, and the resolved exact QA directory plus transferred archive were removed and confirmed absent.

This qualifies Decision 0013 as an implementation-semantic prerequisite for 6G. It does not add mutable buffers, change WVB, or qualify the Windvale-written linker.

## Windvale linker object-scanner qualification

Candidate commit `3eb331a` was archived with SHA-256 `af8c34b304faf258cad811fa3828b127faa99a801cdf3a0a3f3e6f6bd8d63c64`, transferred with the same digest, and verified from that exact archive in `/tmp/windvale-linker-scanner-3eb331a-20260730` on the isolated Debian QA host. Windows and Debian GNU/Linux 12 x64 both completed the Release build with zero warnings/errors, all 33 tests, and the complete native CLI verifier. The QA report identifies .NET `10.0.10`.

The suite compiled and bytecode-verified `Wvˉlinkerˉcore`, checked its exact SHA-256 `ac00a5b702f2a4ef185bd5f021ec2611bd8a335d1937804ceeb30f28cc1b8ded`, exercised embedded valid and malformed WVO cases, accepted representative objects covering all section representations and both relocation kinds, and differentially compared the Windvale scanner with the Stage 0 object oracle for 128 deterministic one-byte mutations plus 128 bounded random byte values. The real CLI verifier proved capability refusal, no hosted read during self-test, acceptance of the Windvale-written 218-byte assembler object, and deterministic rejection of non-WVO source.

The Debian report was retrieved with SHA-256 `824eac7247d83a2d790aba87a80c583b67155cc056a48e5fe73a133bd81d1e67`. Its normalized contract matched the Windows report and included `wvLinkerCoreResult` `0` plus exact hosted output `object status=Valid sections=2 symbols=3 relocations=2 offset=218` followed by LF. The directly retrieved Debian `Wv-Linker-Core.wvb` matched the Windows artifact byte for byte. Every previous module, object, image, and map identity remained unchanged. After evidence retrieval, the previously resolved exact QA directory and transferred archive were removed and confirmed absent.

This qualifies Decision 0014 and the WVO parsing/view portion of roadmap gate 6G. It does not qualify symbol resolution, layout, relocation application, independent image verification, map construction, hosted image publication, the complete Windvale linker, or Phase 6.

## Windvale linker resolution/layout qualification

Candidate commit `709ccb3` was archived with SHA-256 `28740cfcf8d97b54619235b433b4c404ac74fc1d2d56e77e6bbb6e026e8656fd`, transferred with the same digest, and verified from that exact archive in `/tmp/windvale-linker-layout-709ccb3-20260730` on the isolated Debian QA host. Windows and Debian GNU/Linux 12 x64 both completed the Release build with zero warnings/errors, all 34 tests, and the complete native CLI verifier.

The new tests compare Windvale analysis with the Stage 0 oracle for canonical and reversed input order, aligned and deliberately unaligned bases, code/read-only-data/writable-data/zero-fill placement, exact aggregate counts, final image length, entry address, invalid objects and requests, aggregate overflow, duplicate exports, undefined imports, symbol-kind mismatch, missing entry, and address-space overflow. The five-object 320-section aggregate fixture also exposed and then verified removal of a cubic section-name scan; the accepted implementation walks record cursors directly and remains within the normal 20,000,000-instruction verifier budget. Snapshot evidence confirms that repeated semantic passes invoke the native reader once per exact resource name. No success or rejection path writes the reserved image output in this analysis-only slice.

The Debian report was retrieved with SHA-256 `2d02a8af949b80df0778fdf2d486967cd0174b19c4789da390e89b73778e8f28`. Its normalized contract matched the Windows report and included exact Windvale analysis output `link status=Valid inputs=2 sections=3 symbols=4 relocations=2 image-bytes=24 entry-address=1048576 input=4294967295` followed by LF. The directly retrieved Debian `Wv-Linker-Core.wvb` matched the Windows artifact byte for byte at SHA-256 `2a4c24d1330ffbfc6d7253f16978fe5a86264c5118d8bb3e20473d35be023707`. Every previous object, image, and map identity remained unchanged. After evidence retrieval, the resolved exact QA directory and transferred archive were removed and confirmed absent.

This qualifies Decision 0015 and the multi-object resolution/layout portion of roadmap gate 6G. It does not qualify image construction, relocation application, independent image reconstruction, canonical map construction, hosted image publication, the complete Windvale linker, or Phase 6.

## Windvale immutable-image/relocation qualification

Candidate commit `ec9c980` was archived with SHA-256 `a1252652bfef44abedb477f684c265ff222d40a5d24f1a4c7d1da33a1491d6ca`, transferred with the same digest, and verified from that exact archive in `/tmp/windvale-linker-image-ec9c980-20260730` on the isolated Debian QA host. Windvale constructs the canonical 24-byte candidate from two Windvale/Stage 0-identical WVO inputs, including 15 bytes of alignment padding, materialized code and read-only data, an imported relative call target, and an absolute data relocation. The emitted SHA-256 is the Stage 0 oracle identity `0e02d447ec379e8bc8be373694d6ca14fdde0125550cbd34ee05b3ecc63ffe9a`. Additional oracle comparisons cover reversed semantic input order, deliberately unaligned bases, writable data, BSS, local/export targets, negative relative values, and both relocation kinds. Its normalized contract matched the Windows report, including the complete two-line Windvale image report. After evidence retrieval, the resolved exact QA directory and transferred archive were removed and confirmed absent.

This qualifies Decision 0016 and the candidate-image/relocation portion of roadmap gate 6G. It does not qualify independent image reconstruction, canonical map construction, hosted image publication, the complete Windvale linker, or Phase 6.

## Windvale independent-image-reconstruction qualification

Candidate commit `d8008e3` was archived with SHA-256 `9f1d73966581f1181c69144dca1503830e0d83f65f587eca53f5ee580fb6eb12`, transferred with the same digest, and verified from that exact archive in `/tmp/windvale-linker-verify-d8008e377e7b-20260730` on the isolated Debian QA host. Windows and Debian GNU/Linux 12 x64 both completed the Release build with zero warnings/errors, all 34 tests, and the complete native CLI verifier. The QA report identifies .NET `10.0.10`.

The independently structured Windvale verifier rebuilt section padding, materialized data, BSS, provider resolutions, defined-symbol addresses, and both relocation kinds without calling the production image builder. It scanned all symbols for exports, applied relocations in reverse input/source order, used separate signed-magnitude arithmetic, and compared every byte. Embedded fault injection required `WVL1011` plus an empty result for a corrupted reconstruction. A valid 4 MiB code-plus-BSS image exercised the accepted maximum image size under the explicit 200,000,000-instruction ceiling while still invoking the reader once per input and the writer zero times.

The Debian report was retrieved with SHA-256 `eeb5c34026dbec92d20e3cf26e5c486fcb062774039414c4998b109687c96e0e`. Its complete normalized contract matched the Windows report, including the exact two-line canonical analysis output and image identity `0e02d447ec379e8bc8be373694d6ca14fdde0125550cbd34ee05b3ecc63ffe9a`. The directly retrieved Debian `Wv-Linker-Core.wvb` matched the Windows artifact byte for byte at SHA-256 `0b8d4ce09a043a675e64018c02fac94740b0b878a74801fc622ce7703e956b35`. Every previous object, image, and map identity remained unchanged. The reserved Windvale output remained absent on success and rejection because canonical map construction is not yet implemented. After evidence retrieval, the resolved exact QA directory and transferred archive were removed and confirmed absent.

This qualifies Decision 0017 and the independent complete-image reconstruction portion of roadmap gate 6G. It does not qualify canonical map construction, hosted image publication, the complete Windvale linker, or Phase 6.

## Complete Windvale linker / Phase 6 qualification

Candidate commit `40ac57d` was archived as `artifacts/windvale-linker-complete-40ac57d1b2ff.tar.gz` with SHA-256 `2f8e0e570370cb91a3e35f16ae3341095f6963ecc23eaaaf41f7fb83d0a629f3`, transferred with the same digest, and verified from that exact archive in `/tmp/windvale-linker-complete-40ac57d1b2ff-20260730` on the isolated Debian QA host. Windows and Debian GNU/Linux 12 x64 both completed the Release build with zero warnings/errors, all 34 tests, and the complete native CLI verifier. The QA report identifies .NET `10.0.10`.

The complete Windvale linker accepted the same two canonical inputs as Stage 0, independently reconstructed every image byte, built canonical map version 1, invoked the writer exactly once only after complete deterministic success, and produced the exact 24-byte image SHA-256 `0e02d447ec379e8bc8be373694d6ca14fdde0125550cbd34ee05b3ecc63ffe9a` plus the exact 1,721-byte map SHA-256 `31bc6a8e90d5f3049ae3e2eb0735a901923186d6a03ed40f22762b557b2ba5f4`. The qualified WVB SHA-256 is `8d3cb567f6985077b3ad487627bf77a20326b4bc02bcab8d938354f48d339cfd`. The accepted 4 MiB image completed under the explicit 200,000,000-instruction ceiling. Four valid objects containing 16,384 short-name definitions reached the exact canonical-map limit path, returned `WVL1012`, and invoked no writer under the same ceiling.

The Debian report was retrieved with SHA-256 `64798286563e2d626f4e7d4fca2fffbe0feed5417dcf7e0abf9e3d14776ff3ca`; the Windows report SHA-256 is `496ab9faae9b62da3d733bdb62da7bd20fb8980e165a1f6c6b6be3d32d4b221a`. Their normalized contracts matched, including exact equality between the Windvale `wvLinkerMapOutput` and Stage 0 `linkMap` values. The directly retrieved Debian WVB, linked image, and map matched their Windows artifacts byte for byte. The native success path wrote once; every deterministic rejected link invoked no writer and preserved an existing image; and a missing output parent remained the separate host diagnostic `WVR3022`. After evidence retrieval, the previously resolved exact QA directory and transferred archive were removed and confirmed absent.

This qualifies Decision 0018, roadmap gate 6G, and Phase 6. It does not qualify Phase 7 Foundation modules, a self-hosted compiler, native lowering or executable containers, directly executable host tools, firmware, a kernel, drivers, or an operating system.

## Bounded source-module composition / Phase 7 enabling qualification

Candidate commit `df80f91` was archived as `artifacts/windvale-source-modules-df80f91.tar.gz` with SHA-256 `d69de6acee577c2396edd047ec1e09b776ba4e3fb512f087fd2f3e2a3d1c6b8c`, transferred with the same digest, and verified from that exact archive in `/tmp/windvale-source-modules-df80f9154c45-20260730` on the isolated Debian QA host. Windows and Debian GNU/Linux 12 x64 both completed the Release build with zero warnings/errors, all 35 tests, and the complete native CLI verifier. The QA host used .NET SDK `10.0.302` and .NET `10.0.10`.

The qualified compiler resolves a bounded explicit source set, rejects duplicate/missing/cyclic/unreferenced graphs and unsupported dependency shapes, semantically validates each portable dependency against only its own transitive closure, then internalizes dependency functions into one ordinary verified WVB 1.6 module. Reversing the two explicit dependency arguments produced the same exact 339-byte module. Its SHA-256 is `5d27c9667eb66e1abbf46b40d02ab3d4e01b94a421a93bffd0375a550440a612`, its only WVB export is root `Main`, and portable execution returned `42` on both hosts. The real CLI missing-transitive-import case returned `WVC0007`, created no new output, and preserved an existing output unchanged.

The Debian report was retrieved with SHA-256 `4be571b8f73e15b9b1f01302435f89459405ca48e83dacd3c93c22ae1c46686f`; the Windows report SHA-256 is `f943887d661732b9a81ce35723c730430a8e61e90f88771ce8e3046e4d8f21cf`. Their normalized contracts matched, including `sourceCompositionSha256` and `sourceCompositionResult`, and the directly retrieved Debian composition module matched the Windows artifact byte for byte. Every previously qualified module, object, image, and map identity remained unchanged. After evidence retrieval, the resolved exact QA directory and transferred archive were removed and confirmed absent.

This qualifies Decision 0019 and the source-module prerequisite for Phase 7. It does not yet qualify a reusable Foundation API because no shared source module is consumed by both the assembler and linker. It also does not qualify imported data or nominal types, runtime WVB linkage, package discovery, the self-hosted compiler, native tooling, or any OS layer.

## Windvale bootstrap source-lexer qualification

## Windvale bootstrap body-parser qualification

Candidate commit `ddfa9e3` was archived as `windvale-source-body-parser-ddfa9e3.tar.gz` with SHA-256 `6ebc36462fc4ed0655b97d8c10450d81c8e28a3d0d9f305543fd6b4a65e3bda4`, transferred with the same digest, and verified from that exact archive in `/tmp/windvale-source-body-parser-ddfa9e3` on the isolated Debian QA host. Windows and Debian GNU/Linux 12 x64 both completed the Release build with zero warnings/errors, all 42 tests, and the complete native CLI verifier. The QA host used .NET SDK `10.0.302` and .NET `10.0.10`.

The portable body pass implements every Stage 0 statement and expression form over the declaration parser's exact body spans. It returns flat immutable views and retains no token, declaration, statement, expression, or syntax-tree collection. The tests cover all statement/expression forms and precedence, exact child and body spans, lexical/declaration failure propagation, and the hard boundaries for 64 call arguments, expression and statement nesting, 4,096 statements per function, and aggregate expression nodes. The portable demo returned `0` on both hosts under the 30,000,000-instruction ceiling.

Both hosts produced the exact 164,806-byte `Source-Body-Parser.wvb` SHA-256 `bb04309dfd4b037c05a4f0d52903d937336e90e64077fbc1b78cf5ea88c1de5f`, 168,718-byte demo SHA-256 `5c479f4e922852043696a599a7832a4111d326ef54ce8222166caf3570ec28ba`, and 165,942-byte hosted tool SHA-256 `761887d3674833854d976dd394ad3f83f27d2c74748b6dd0f296c97b117140ca`. Directly retrieved Debian copies matched the Windows artifacts byte for byte.

At the conservative qualification ceilings, the hosted tool produced these identical reports on both hosts:

```text
source bodies status=Valid functions=14 top-level=138 statements=510 expression-nodes=1432 statement-depth=17 expression-depth=5 offset=39211
source bodies status=Valid functions=24 top-level=232 statements=527 expression-nodes=2135 statement-depth=5 expression-depth=3 offset=64951
source bodies status=Valid functions=38 top-level=234 statements=519 expression-nodes=2500 statement-depth=5 expression-depth=3 offset=69023
```

These cover the real lexer at 100,000,000 instructions, declaration parser at 160,000,000, and body parser at 160,000,000. ## Canonical packed compiler source-set qualification

Candidate commit `00ef0b1` was archived as `windvale-source-set-00ef0b1.tar.gz`, 315,314 bytes with SHA-256 `0628a6666d8afdb27db963930c7aba2bb02c8430f40d9eacea7e5607e652f53a`, transferred with the same digest, and verified from that exact archive in `/tmp/windvale-source-set-00ef0b1` on the isolated Debian QA host. The portable source-set module validates the complete WVSS 1 envelope before exposing any source slice; accepts one through 64 nonempty sources; rejects bad headers, truncation, noncanonical layout, trailing bytes, source or body syntax failures, duplicate declared names, nonordinal dependencies, and invalid dependency profiles/shapes; and keeps import-graph and symbol binding in their later compiler phases. Tests include the exact 64-module boundary and prove that repeated hosted resource names use one first-successful-read snapshot. ## Validated scan reuse and ten-module closure qualification

Decision 0055 retains checked standalone lexer/parser entry points while letting compiler loops consume already established full-source scan and cursor evidence. Source-set validation reuses its declaration summary for the body pass. A bounded byte scanner skips lexically valid function bodies while tracking strings, escapes, comments, braces, line/column coordinates, and the existing depth limit; rejected or ambiguous shapes fall back to the qualified token scanner. The checked body-span boundary adds an iterative nesting preflight so adversarial depth returns `Nestingˉlimit` without host-stack exhaustion. Nominal lookup uses the existing WVSI reverse table and first-byte canonical ranges.

The focused typed-WVIR fixture falls from Decision 0050's 5,715,847 instructions to 3,626,693, a reduction of 2,089,154 instructions or 36.55%. The focused 24-test compiler area passed in 37.927 seconds. The complete candidate Windows Standard suite passed all 48 tests in 246.417 seconds, and focused nesting-containment and golden-contract reruns also passed.

The exact ten-module typed-WVIR input now completes below the unchanged 4,000,000,000-instruction ceiling:

```text
source wir status=Valid modules=10 functions=315 blocks=5658 operations=27377 temporaries=25094 operands=21869 directory-bytes=1720804
Result: 0
Instructions: 3912239584
```

The retained margin is 87,760,416 instructions. This clears the performance entry gate for Stage 0 → Stage 1 → Stage 2 convergence but does not itself prove compiler self-hosting, byte-for-byte bootstrap convergence, closure of the 4 MiB source envelope, completion of Phase 8, native tooling, or any OS layer.

## Checked native i32 arithmetic and trap qualification

Decision 0060 advances the experimental shared target to `x86-64-wvb-baseline-v2` and ABI 2. Verified straight-line WVB constants and single-assignment i32 temporaries lower to numbered native values, checked add/subtract/multiply/negate operations, and one return. The x86-64 selector uses a bounded one-page frame, emits `jo` after every checked operation, and returns a packed 64-bit value/status word so every successful `i32` bit pattern remains representable. Status 1 becomes recoverable Windvale trap `WVR3007`. The fragment verifier independently decodes the entire constant or arithmetic shape, including the minimal frame, contiguous initialized slots, exact instruction encodings, overflow targets, balanced epilogues, packed status, symbol partition, and complete byte consumption before either WVO or W^X publication.

The differential computation `-(((2 + 2) - (7 * 6)) - 4)` returns `42` in the reference interpreter, W^X JIT fragment, and WVO-linked AOT image. Its 239 code bytes have SHA-256 `9ab91125f773fc56f6aafc4ac66ebb4596d2dc93f7eec713d13aaf1053558bf6`; its deterministic 341-byte WVO has SHA-256 `ef8bb7e4322ef7bce4ad9de0710a441f617bfb6dbc5e7d05b3e883fce1319cbc`. The full `i32` result range includes successful `int.MinValue`; separate add, subtract, multiply, and negate programs trap in the interpreter and native path, the add trap survives WVO linking, and five structural corruption families are rejected as `WVN3030` before execution.

## Dynamic native instruction-budget and backward-control qualification

Decision 0062 advances the experimental shared target to `x86-64-wvb-baseline-v4` and ABI 4. Every WVB instruction lowers with one explicit native charge; explicit terminators consume a charge while implicit fallthrough does not invent one. The executor supplies `(0, budget, budget)`, giving the same dynamic positive maximum in `RDX` under Windows and System V x64; the fragment reserves `R11`, permits exactly that many charges, and returns packed status 2 as `WVR3011` before the next instruction. The independently decoded fragment requires exact budget initialization, strict charge/semantic alternation, limit targets, both trap epilogues, control targets on charge boundaries, complete cyclic reachability, minimal initialized frames, and complete byte consumption.

A finite loop returns `42` through the reference interpreter, Windows/Linux W^X JIT fragment, and exact WVO-linked AOT image at its exact 157-instruction requirement; all paths reject 156 as `WVR3011`. A genuinely nonterminating verified loop is bounded at 50 instructions in both the interpreter and native path. The deterministic loop fragment is 1,178 bytes with SHA-256 `8b4cde486874a5dcd7eded18e0d3fcb208e244de0499e868dc8e50501bd21139`; its 1,318-byte WVO has SHA-256 `7b0d7898a83eb87818b08dfcb4081ce6ff05e91f5dd2958ba0e869ebcae735ac`. Corrupt budget sources, charge amounts, limit targets, limit statuses, and non-charge branch targets fail before publication.

## Shared-budget native calls and static-data qualification

## Borrowed-byte and unsigned native-value qualification

Decision 0066 advances the experimental shared target to `x86-64-wvb-baseline-v7` and ABI 7. Every native local and temporary owns one zero-initialized 16-byte value cell. Scalars use the low dword; an immutable borrowed-byte value uses an exact pointer/length/reserved descriptor. Static byte data, length, checked slicing, all four fixed-width little-endian reads, `u8`/`u32` comparisons, widening, and checked `u32` arithmetic lower through the same native IR and selector into the W^X and WVO/AOT sinks. Borrowed bytes may pass through as many as four internal parameter positions by descriptor pointer and immediate callee copy. Byte range failure returns packed status 6 / `WVR3008` without a host signal.

The focused differential program returns `42` through the reference interpreter, actual W^X native execution, and linked WVO/AOT while exercising every admitted byte read, a slice, length, a borrowed-byte internal parameter, scalar result, unsigned comparison, widening, and checked arithmetic. The independent decoder rejects corrupt data targets, descriptor lengths, descriptor slot types, byte argument forms, and byte-bounds targets before publication. Separate programs prove exact `WVR3008` bounds and `WVR3007` unsigned-overflow agreement.

## Borrowed hosted-input and first native WVB inspector qualification

Decision 0067 advances the experimental shared target to `x86-64-wvb-baseline-v8` and ABI 8. Borrowed text and bytes share a pointer/length/reserved descriptor in zero-initialized 16-byte value cells. Service-table version 2 adds explicitly authorized bounded argument count, argument text, and file snapshot input beside console output. One execution owner validates, caches, and releases every host-returned buffer; the reference and native adapters share file-snapshot limits and exact `WVR302x` failures. Exact Windows/System V thunks preserve one platform-neutral generated-code convention, and the independent decoder validates all descriptor and service-load shapes before WVO or W^X publication.

The checked-in `Examples/Foundation/Wvb-Header-Inspector.wv` accepts one filename, prints the host-returned text, reads a real compiler-produced WVB twice through one adapter snapshot, validates `WVB1` and version `1.6`, prints `wvb-header=pass`, and returns zero under both the reference interpreter and native execution. The focused tests also prove deterministic native code, canonical service metadata, corruption rejection, and agreement for out-of-range arguments (`WVR3020`) and missing files (`WVR3022`).

Exact candidate commit `d970c2789058d37c7059264fcbd76e164a01a939`, tree `1723f1ee8342e36e3237f14b21927e334c9bddae`, was published to both configured remotes. ## Bounded native nominal values and structural wvdump qualification

Decision 0068 advances the implemented target to `x86-64-wvb-baseline-v9` and ABI 9. Enums use canonical signed dword values with nominal identity. Immutable records use 32-bit offsets into one checked 1 MiB execution arena and retain one complete 16-byte typed cell per field. Context version 2 carries arena pointer/length/used fields; exhaustion is packed status 7 / `WVR3017`. Service-table version 3 adds capability-free strict `Textˉutf8ˉisˉvalid` support through exact platform thunks.

The existing structural portion of `Examples/Foundation/Wv-Dump-Core.wv`, through `Inspectˉwvbˉpayloads`, now runs as the same verified native fragment under W^X JIT and linked WVO/AOT. It validates the full canonical seven-section fixture and agrees exactly with the reference interpreter. `Scanˉshape` now encodes its two boolean policies in one bounded `u32`, preserving behavior within the existing four-register call ABI. This is the real checked-in parser, not a parallel toy implementation.

The focused enum/record test covers construction, fields, parameters, returns, equality, inequality, deterministic WVO, corruption rejection, zero-default policy, and arena exhaustion. A separate valid/invalid UTF-8 case proves the pure runtime service with no capability authorization. The ABI-9 portable WVO is 7,946 bytes with SHA-256 `94d10d60f5d37cdab3d0f0c3678ee1e86b312564c06d633baf4736939595fed2`; the version-4 bridge object is 315 bytes with SHA-256 `949c5fdd641722c541e2ed6583a5c28bd681b77d91f28ea5cda2999026d75a23`; the firmware-probe-11 image remains 15,872 bytes with SHA-256 `100070e26666bfc97d0fff8da42d996249d072b5771014838a376abfc0a13d6a`.

## Dynamic native text and complete wvdump qualification

Decision 0069 advances the implemented target to `x86-64-wvb-baseline-v10` and ABI 10 while retaining execution-context version 2. Service-table version 4 is 96 bytes and adds authorized diagnostics plus pure enum naming, invariant integer formatting, bounded concatenation, and deterministic quoting. One execution-owned 16 MiB text arena backs dynamic results; individual text retains the WVB 1 MiB limit (`WVR3012`) and aggregate exhaustion becomes `WVR3018`. Strict `Textˉfromˉutf8` uses the proven validation service and packed status 8 / `WVR3014` for malformed input.

Descriptor-returning callers pass one verified result-cell address in `RAX`; callees save it in a dedicated final frame cell, copy the complete descriptor on success, and preserve packed trap propagation. Void calls use the same status convention without a result. The independent fragment decoder admits only the exact prologue, call, return, service, provenance, and failure shapes and cross-checks caller/callee return kinds.

The complete 1,441-line checked-in `Examples/Foundation/Wv-Dump-Core.wv` now compiles without slicing, inspects a real compiler-produced WVB, and emits byte-identical full reports and diagnostics under the reference interpreter, Windows/Linux W^X JIT, and linked WVO/AOT. Focused negative cases cover invalid UTF-8, aggregate text exhaustion, and corrupt hidden-result metadata. The service-free OS consumer advances to kernel probe version 5 and firmware probe 12. ## First runtime-native UTF-8 service qualification

Decision 0070 retains qualified ABI 10 and service-table version 4 while replacing the `Textˉutf8ˉisˉvalid` managed delegate and Windows/System V adapters with one identical runtime-owned x86-64 leaf. Its internal inputs remain the proven `R8` address, bounded `R9D` length, and verified `RCX` Boolean cell; it writes normalized zero/one, returns status zero, and leaves the resource/context registers untouched.

The leaf is exactly 800 bytes with SHA-256 `4c3d2e370d62c8d2f54a3c453f39b94cf46ddabd6db3c2f3d6b65f0713b68aaf`. Windows and Linux W^X execution agree with the managed strict-decoder oracle over 23 ranges covering empty/ASCII input, every canonical multibyte lead family and boundary, overlong forms, surrogate encodings, truncation, bad continuations, stray continuations, and the `U+10FFFF` ceiling. Rebuilding the leaf is byte-deterministic, and a one-byte corruption fails identity validation before W^X publication. The complete native dynamic-text case continues to pass through strict `Textˉfromˉutf8` conversion.

Exact commit `53cee69bdd24e96bb42521d53e9d55b0c59c5876`, tree `2e6aa434d5956be489fa24f3df8da0e9fc25ec9d`, was archived as 2,877,521 bytes with SHA-256 `de6810995b67e7f8eb678f0ca228ca1c774c1b1ce901c104f8f42646bda55133`. The 15,563-byte Windows report has SHA-256 `c34a2199e548631323b2186dda0dcf8ffcb0a3a3c6eb7d53d9a405c314837a4b`; its 11,915-byte timing report has SHA-256 `81a90a749bf1e651ed0415c449e9c23f1e35c1a575e8090046140eeb030f5058`. ## Final pure runtime native services qualification

Decision 0072 retains ABI 11, context version 3, service-table version 4, WVB 1.6, WVO 1.0, kernel bridge 6, and firmware probe 13 while moving the final two deterministic pure services off managed callback and platform-thunk paths. `Enumˉname` uses one exact 323-byte platform-neutral x86-64 leaf with SHA-256 `fb05590c5b6e1791380ba288c4112387e791a18722428c90276796bd409d130a`. It reads an adjacent bounded runtime-private `WVEN` version-1 block reconstructed from the fragment's independently verified canonical types. The complete leaf-plus-metadata bundle is independently parsed, reconstructed, and byte-compared before W^X publication.

`Textˉquote` uses one exact 1,165-byte platform-neutral x86-64 leaf with SHA-256 `4f334af9b6349437d36fd703edb6b5882416f033fae47906a40a4bafdc083bb7`. Its first pass strictly validates UTF-8, measures the existing UTF-16-code-unit escape representation, and proves the 1 MiB value and 16 MiB arena limits before allocation; its second pass writes the exact ASCII result. Differential coverage includes every short escape, remaining controls, printable ASCII boundaries, BMP Unicode, both supplementary boundaries, multiple enum types, deterministic bundle reconstruction, corrupt metadata, value-limit failure, and separate enum/quote arena exhaustion.

## Bounded native stack-argument and probe-21 qualification

Decision 0089 replaces the current fragment target with ABI 16 while retaining execution-context version 7 and service-table version 5. Positions zero through three keep the prior register convention. Each later position receives one exact 16-byte outgoing cell; the source-language maximum of 64 parameters therefore bounds a call-site reservation to 960 bytes. The caller releases that reservation before packed-status propagation, and the callee copies every later scalar or descriptor into its own initialized frame.

Focused Windows differential evidence passes the maximum 64-parameter scalar call, a fifth-position borrowed-byte descriptor with descriptor return, and a five-parameter void call through the reference interpreter, actual W^X execution, and linked WVO/AOT. A second compilation is byte-identical. The independent fragment verifier rejects a changed maximum reservation, first outgoing-cell displacement, descriptor high-word destination, and release size as `WVN3030`.

The fresh zero-warning Windows Standard gate on the integrated Decisions 0088 through 0090 tree passes all 67 Seed tests in 237.692 seconds. The new case takes 39 milliseconds and the golden closure remains dominant at 177.231 seconds. All 43 compiler-area tests passed in the earlier focused sweep. All 21 current OS tests also pass, including the later fixed WVB-admission slice. The service-free probe's at-most-four-parameter code and WVO remain unchanged by the ABI extension; probe-21 firmware identities change only because Decision 0090 adds admission artifacts and evidence. The 15,798-byte candidate report has SHA-256 `310b2b003caf04e257bd14d9ae614f2bf02668fc5f6df0c26d9267ba4d1c25b1`; its 13,729-byte timing report has SHA-256 `dd663af1fa49004896174d195d822b9d088226ca3d1c8c72d61dc917d8220053`.

The exact 599,868-byte compiler WVB contains 328 functions: 103 require five through 23 parameters. ABI 16 passes the former eight-parameter blocker and all wider signatures before preflight stops at `WVN2002` in `Compilerˉsourceˉwirˉcompileˉblock`. That function has 11 supported parameters, 1,049 locals, and declared stack depth 12 against the native frame's current 1,024-slot cap. It is the compiler's only function at or above 1,024 locals; observed maxima are 1,049 locals, stack depth 34, and locals-plus-declared-stack depth 1,061. The next slice must measure lowered numbered-value pressure before choosing a larger fixed frame or deterministic slot reuse.

It does not qualify native compiler execution, frame-bound expansion, register allocation, a general guest verifier/loader, process isolation, standalone host tools, Hyper-V, or .NET retirement.

After evidence retrieval and comparison, the resolved exact Debian QA directory, transferred source archive, and remote evidence bundle were removed and confirmed absent. The exact local archive, Windows artifacts, and retrieved Debian evidence bundle remain retained.

## Probe-23 cross-host build and Seed evidence

Exact commit `22e350b8965bbe70452261dabfc411d28cf7a1d5` adds the first Windvale init/resource service, two protected CPL3 roots, reduced send/receive endpoints, deterministic block/wake coordination, and contained client fault evidence. GitHub [Verify run 30730151722](https://github.com/eworker-inc/Windvale/actions/runs/30730151722) passes classification plus the Windows and Linux verification jobs.

Both jobs restore and compile the complete solution, including `Windvale.Bootstrap` and `Windvale.Os.Tests`, then pass all 67 Seed qualification tests. The Windows suite reports 217.039 seconds; Linux reports 208.599 seconds. This is valid cross-host build and Seed evidence for the exact source state.

The workflow does not execute the `Windvale.Os.Tests` binary. Probe 23's 25 focused OS tests and four pinned-QEMU scenarios pass on Windows, but a Linux run of the OS-test binary from the same exact source state has not been recorded. Probe 23 therefore remains a candidate rather than replacing exact commit `860c69c`/probe 21 as the latest fully cross-host-qualified OS baseline.

## Probe-24 cross-host Seed and OS qualification

Exact commit `190174a01299369fb855e27ea676d34062e09c5b` adds the first user-space Windvale bytecode interpreter and integrates the focused OS suite into every regular non-Fast repository verification. The client process executes the admitted 174-byte WVB through its AOT-built Windvale interpreter at CPL3; the admitted program's host-built AOT derivative is absent from that computation path. The separate init service, two roots, reduced endpoints, deterministic wake, contained client fault, and terminal kernel faults remain composed.

GitHub [Verify run 30732061301](https://github.com/eworker-inc/Windvale/actions/runs/30732061301) passes classification plus the complete Windows and digest-pinned Debian 12 verification jobs for that exact source state. Each host passes all 67 Seed tests and all 25 OS tests. Windows Seed elapsed time is 247.422 seconds and Debian Seed elapsed time is 145.020 seconds. This promotes probe 24 over probe 21 as the latest fully cross-host-qualified OS checkpoint and qualifies the retained probe-22/probe-23 process and init-service composition.

Pinned Windows QEMU 11.0/Q35/TCG supplies the machine evidence: the 114,176-byte normal image SHA-256 `4248f3402a0abfe9eda531109460fb37a7c1f3f907ded162a49a785bab38fbb7` exits 0; the same-sized invalid-opcode image SHA-256 `465ac8d095160e169187860413b0b347148df4acc2f24802f7a5e166981f6c59` and general-protection image SHA-256 `0a65ba2982f551434553054fc92d82a1a6e727a73527c85a79526f328105b58c` exit 3; and the 114,688-byte contained-interpreter-fault image SHA-256 `8cd17f693ae088eefaccb2b8449fd47f34e345352bb46beebd4a2a32fe8fad5d` exits 0 after the independent init service completes. The cross-host workflow does not run QEMU on Debian, so it qualifies deterministic construction and all OS contract tests on both hosts without claiming duplicate Linux emulator evidence.

This evidence qualifies the fixed-offset interpreter profile, not arbitrary WVB input, general section discovery, a complete verifier/interpreter, JIT publication, scheduling, process lifecycle, Hyper-V, physical hardware, or .NET retirement. Decision 0094 begins the next candidate section-derived profile from this exact checkpoint.

Exact commit `33555fdc4305f457638431ddbc40cb79fafa51c3` advances the CPL3 Windvale interpreter from fixed serialized offsets to checked WVB 1.6 envelope traversal, seven ordered section payloads, and one bounded function/export profile. The process contract records interpreter profile 2, a 32-page RX image, four-page RW/NX stack, one RW/NX context page, exact instruction/call-depth budgets, and policy token 94. ## Probe-26 runtime-supplied WVB qualification
