## Probe-27 init-owned resource-grant qualification

Qualified Decision 0096 moves the first real boot-resource selection into Windvale init without changing the admitted WVB, interpreter, ABI-16 service leaf, or result channel. Protected-process version 6 places the exact 174-byte WVB in an init-owned RO/NX page. Process `2` begins with its target PTE absent and context service/resource pointers zero. Init's 273-byte Windvale WVB selects resource identifier `1`; its WVA shim invokes fixed grant syscall `4`, then waits for the existing result.

The grant validates one kernel-owned 128-byte `WVRES001` record and the exact owner/client process state. It installs one RO/NX alias, publishes service-table version 5 and `WVBR` version 1 in the client data page, changes owned state to borrowed, records borrower `2`, and sets grant/mapping counts to one. Init remains lifetime owner. Replay, changed owner bytes, a pre-mapped target, an out-of-range service leaf, changed WVB bytes, or a changed digest fails closed.

Exact implementation commit `4701200e707786f04d4462bb75a1664cd5ed13cc` passes GitHub [Verify run 30739172682](https://github.com/eworker-inc/Windvale/actions/runs/30739172682). Windows and digest-pinned Debian 12 build with zero warnings/errors, pass all 67 Seed tests and all 25 OS tests, and complete the non-Fast verifier. The Seed suites take 236.791 seconds on Windows and 190.185 seconds on Linux. The focused Windows suite also passes all 25 OS tests. Deterministic qualified identities include process-policy WVB `fad470d9988c997daf4e44f90bbfe665391f5f02dd84ba8e8025580efc11c49f` (4,610 bytes), init WVB `0fe423c499ce4f573095ddb9ff03355ee8b6ad927941f764ddaf2eaf9537f78b` (273 bytes), linked init image `ba2a2abe03d420506c79af61cc917f4b0124a2ad7687fa80117e353dde475727` (1,385 bytes), and normal process-machine WVO `d863e61be67659b30b370da8ba9174b712f0d0bd8f02f31b9cdbb9fd523334c3` (137,807 bytes).

Pinned Windows QEMU 11.0/Q35/TCG passes all four exact probe-27 scenarios. The 224,768-byte normal image SHA-256 `709ebd7f643f2f9d9c7cf4eb4042977c675a3ff19d7a34da4d7e26e0526a29b7` exits 0; the same-sized invalid-opcode image SHA-256 `a89e66da871fcf46637ce4d91463268b2c8cce4309d12953eb7c7b464f57178f` and general-protection image SHA-256 `ab10c10cc0af01ebe5603033d2f35bce86660b23953c33ff6012ad7cee83a1c5` exit 3; and the 225,280-byte contained-user-fault image SHA-256 `b5e726b51f26f48cc9948095bfce4eabaf0b3bc90b89a6c3b3650325adfb05bb` exits 0. Normal and contained-fault serial evidence includes `resource-grant=pass` only after the real CPL3 init grant and completes interpretation, IPC, init exit, and shutdown.

This evidence qualifies probe 27's fixed grant on Windows and Linux; QEMU boot execution remains Windows-only evidence. It does not qualify resource names, multiple resources/recipients, general capability transfer, ownership migration, revocation, teardown, reclamation, packages, arbitrary loading, broader semantics, JIT, scheduling, Hyper-V, physical hardware, or .NET retirement.

## Qualified probe-28 terminal resource-borrow cleanup

Qualified Decision 0097 advances protected processes to `WVPROC07`, the 128-byte resource record to `WVRES002`, Windvale policy token `96`, and firmware probe 28 while retaining memory version 5, paging version 3, ABI 16/context 7/service table 5, interpreter profile 4, both user images, the admitted WVB, and the WVA resource leaf byte-identically.

After process `2` exits or reaches the contained vector-13 fault, the process machine revalidates the live immutable borrow. It accepts the exact granted leaf with or without x86-64 accessed bit 5, which hardware sets during the interpreter's WVB read, and rejects every other mutation. It then clears the client PTE, both native-context resource pointers, the complete 104-byte service table, and the complete 32-byte `WVBR` table. The resource returns to owned/no-borrower state with grant count one and mapping count zero; init's physical page, WVB bytes, and digest remain owned and unchanged. Reloading init's CR3 flushes non-global client translations on the current single-CPU path; the client root is retired rather than reused.

The focused Windows suite passes all 25 OS tests. Deterministic coverage proves byte-identical normal, repeated, accessed-leaf, and contained-fault cleanup; rejects nonterminal/mixed state as `WVOS6201`, malformed live records as `WVOS6202`, inconsistent aliases/publication as `WVOS6203`, and a released-record replay; and retains every earlier deterministic artifact and malformed-input check. Qualified identities include policy WVB `c4aacb9036f825ecd3d038954c1d07c573b43eb4f6ee831d0b81d188f0682679` (5,152 bytes), policy WVO `d6290156c6d7cf709ba44b3035fac4eea75995ecf3ddfbcb8a0a9b73c0612509` (45,886 bytes), normal process-machine WVO `19da37cdc044505a92410449a14e72c35ed8573b409c095b0b9a8a8f9d21f065` (138,751 bytes), and fault process-machine WVO `bedd2a06969d3df295efe8eefa626dd1c97737731ef1056fbcb6df152f259138` (138,783 bytes).

Pinned Windows QEMU 11.0/Q35/TCG passes all four exact probe-28 scenarios with `resource-revoked=pass`. The 230,912-byte normal image SHA-256 `bc5f04c0e75fb217c9339bcc2a391bbe68f9f79ad97c18a93e35e310dab62d46` exits 0; the same-sized invalid-opcode image `2c9c6c60543d3729f7401720c0b03e98dc2c5e3654e45668bfcd4559650bc543` and general-protection image `bdccbd123dd457d88c4902f33e727320b08c93e6acd62ac0f706912d5f1163ca` exit 3; and the 231,424-byte contained-user-fault image `221c710d741565c7113a7b8c2ea94c66358018e144ae59f583a3c1ce10225494` exits 0.

Exact implementation commit `b2197fa4fb78b26d75e4fd5269cde590cbd98dcf` passes GitHub [Verify run 30741650532](https://github.com/eworker-inc/Windvale/actions/runs/30741650532). Windows and digest-pinned Debian 12 each pass all 67 Seed tests, all 25 OS tests, and the complete non-Fast verifier. Seed elapsed time is 237.598 seconds on Windows and 210.262 seconds on Linux. This qualified probe 28 as the then-latest cross-host OS baseline. It proves one automatic terminal borrow cleanup, not explicit revocation, page or root reclamation/reuse, SMP shootdown, multiple resources or recipients, ownership transfer, arbitrary loading, JIT publication, scheduling, Hyper-V, physical hardware, or .NET retirement.

## Qualified probe-29 typed two-resource lookup

Qualified Decision 0098 advances protected processes to `WVPROC08`, resource records to two `WVRES003` entries, the private directory to `WVBR002`, memory to `WVKMEM06`, Windvale policy token to `97`, and firmware to probe 29. ABI 16/context 7/service table 5, WVA seam 8, admission bridge 2, retained bridge 10, paging 3, and interpreter profile number 4 remain active.

Init owns two distinct pages: the exact admitted WVB under `boot:main.wvb`, and four little-endian bytes encoding budget `4` under `boot:main.budget`. Its Windvale `Main` selects ordered set token `131073`. The kernel accepts no unknown, duplicate, reversed, or partial set; validates both source pages, digests, records, and absent target PTEs; then installs both aliases and publishes the 80-byte typed directory atomically. The WVA-owned 347-byte leaf validates one of the two exact entries. The Windvale interpreter charges one unit for each of the canonical four opcodes; budget `3` exhausts before return. Exit and contained fault revalidate and clear both aliases and the complete publication while preserving init ownership and one historical grant per record.

Pinned QEMU exposed a machine-only stack bound that the reference models could not show. The enlarged Windvale process policy overwrote state on the old two-page kernel stack; a measured three-page configuration still did not complete process construction; four pages pass the complete composition. Memory version 6 therefore uses a 63-page arena: state page 0, kernel stack pages 1–4, IDT page 5, paging pages 6–11, init pages 12–20, and client pages 21–62. The process coordinator reconstructs the 2 MiB-aligned arena base from the owned stack and revalidates `WVKMEM06` after the policy call.

The local Windows focused suite passes all 25 OS tests. Coverage includes exact/repeated grant and cleanup, ordinary exit, contained fault, hardware accessed bits, distinct source pages/PTEs, exact `WVBR002` entries, budget success/exhaustion/bounds/malformed/missing cases, and rejection of token, budget kind/length/digest/page, directory, PTE, record, and service mutations. Deterministic qualified identities include process-policy WVB `5134108d706aefd18ac90c18cefe793d9ec166f19066484219e2618300e4cedb` (6,955 bytes), interpreter WVB `7fbb25fe08136c86c063c08395451f8db1219bd17e0adc0748b5fa2d9a3f8fee` (12,851 bytes), normal process-machine WVO `b18ccf8f4c2eb065d017e2fafb2254fbf0299af1cf0eb130c3ca3405a34392e2` (149,483 bytes), and fault process-machine WVO `a3bc89dbdd1539934417ae03f4375bd36af1ef6617d1b4f7651b5955f923ebd0` (149,531 bytes).

Pinned Windows QEMU 11.0/Q35/TCG passes all four clean Probe 29 scenarios with `typed-resources=pass`. The 258,048-byte normal image `a8a14581eab4c1a6d67aba7af0cec1baa956574a410a4cd0de1121e1f843ee67` exits 0; the same-sized invalid-opcode image `35ee08e97aff4f6a2c0018c962960d5c7ee8af58fe6d5b36565613a99292ad0f` and general-protection image `92ae33986ab53245f57dc9263179e6dcd2c66cf79b634dcedaee51e93f915ca7` exit 3; and the 258,560-byte contained-user-fault image `35a3dece4e64463bc9df7ef73c83ec5f5fff3b0daedd7176f77f1c2ef5525484` exits 0.

Exact implementation commit `3fd9ef7535d7536ed084144e4f697cda548bf35c` passes GitHub [Verify run 30745623111](https://github.com/eworker-inc/Windvale/actions/runs/30745623111). Windows and digest-pinned Debian 12 each pass all 67 Seed tests, all 25 OS tests, and the complete non-Fast verifier. Seed elapsed time is 229.159 seconds on Windows and 196.686 seconds on Linux. Both qualification logs emit the same 56 SHA-256 values in exact order. This qualifies Decision 0098 and Probe 29 as the latest cross-host OS baseline. It proves neither a dynamic namespace nor arbitrary counts, independent resource lifetimes, ownership transfer, page/root reuse, scheduling, JIT publication, Hyper-V, physical hardware, nor .NET retirement; QEMU execution remains Windows-only evidence.

## Qualified ABI-17 frame admission and Probe-30 root reuse

Qualified Decision 0099 advances the current target to `x86-64-wvb-baseline-v17` while retaining context version 7, service-table version 5, WVB 1.6, WVO 1.0, and ABI 16's 64-parameter convention. The hard frame envelope is now 2,048 16-byte cells. Exact compiler preflight passes the former `Compilerˉsourceˉwirˉcompileˉblock` local-count failure and reaches `WVN2004` in `Compilerˉbodyˉparseˉprimary`: the function requests at least 2,049 combined local/value slots. The improved diagnostic records the exact function, requirement, and limit. This is progress toward native compiler execution, not completion of it.

Qualified Decision 0100 advances memory to `WVKMEM07`, protected processes to `WVPROC09`, resource records to `WVRES004`, and firmware to Probe 30. Generation-1 process reference `65538` and generation-2 reference `131074` may use the same physical root but cannot share logical identity. After the first terminal cleanup, an exact LIFO operation accepts only the 42-page allocator tail, restores cursor/free state from `63/0` to `21/42`, zeroes every byte, and immediately reallocates the identical root. Both resource records preserve grant history across the release; the second grant advances it to `2`, and stale generation-1 process/resource evidence is rejected.

The focused Windows suite passes all 25 OS tests. It covers zero/non-tail release rejection, full zeroing and same-address reallocation, both generation planners/grants/cleanups, stale-generation rejection, deterministic artifacts, and the complete retained malformed-input suite. Pinned Windows QEMU 11.0/Q35/TCG passes all four Probe-30 scenarios with `process-reuse=pass`: normal `5034c01a98f20344d96fa091fd9a55a303e72669d746a4b83df2900eed93992f` (261,120 bytes, exit 0), invalid opcode `bb57ebf7e50eb56bf3d42d91b2213ed5b262554416fdf76609142eccba44cc55` (261,120 bytes, exit 3), general protection `d56fe572fb7a7ff724f7b7c26aa5299a6c5cee4c203f009b63d651c1d3cd8fcc` (261,120 bytes, exit 3), and contained user fault `78dfa73a80a05021273cb44587f6b957d16d4cd4ebaec487f7b8a8f5427846ca` (261,632 bytes, exit 0).

Exact implementation commit `4a077ab9ebaf2108201927eef3095e87ef2ed907` passes GitHub [Verify run 30749304867](https://github.com/eworker-inc/Windvale/actions/runs/30749304867). Windows and digest-pinned Debian 12 each pass all 67 Seed tests, all 25 OS tests, and the complete non-Fast verifier. Seed elapsed time is 221.700 seconds on Windows and 201.079 seconds on Linux. Both qualification logs emit the same 56 SHA-256 values in exact order. This qualifies Decisions 0099 and 0100, ABI 17, and Probe 30 as the latest cross-host native/OS baselines. QEMU execution remains Windows-only evidence, and neither decision claims native compiler execution, a general allocator, scheduling, JIT publication, Hyper-V, physical hardware, or .NET retirement.

## Qualified Probe-31 exact-WVB portability proof

Qualified Decision 0101 advances admission to version 3, interpreter profile to 5, kernel memory to `WVKMEM08`, paging to `WVKPAG04`, protected processes to `WVPROC10`, and firmware to Probe 31 while retaining ABI 17/context 7/service table 5, WVA seam 8, admission bridge 2, retained bridge 10, `WVRES004`, and `WVBR002`.

The admitted resource is the exact 493-byte canonical WVB compiled from `Examples/Seed/Sum-Data.wv`, SHA-256 `6f3a272d37dd8893995c7f85c236414ed2864bf59de2f3775c08afd426013f8c`. Its immutable `[3,5,8,13]` data, internal `Add`, loop, locals, branches, and calls execute for exactly 203 guest instructions and return `29` through the reference runtime, the shared Windows/Linux native backend, and both protected Windvale OS client generations. The AOT interpreter consumes exactly 93,181 native instructions at dynamic depth 4; its largest frame is 1,883 slots under the unchanged ABI-17 limit of 2,048. Each client uses a 256-byte record arena inside its existing RW/NX data page and consumes exactly 240 bytes.

The focused Windows suite passes all 25 OS tests. Pinned Windows QEMU 11.0/Q35/TCG passes all four exact Probe-31 scenarios: normal `30acb028e44b6d12bc4d0e4d34232d86a43b83b40f070d3a48b7c56e505bc0bc` (531,456 bytes, exit 0), invalid opcode `dec5a39be132a3e6f140425547097b450a38a7b62e6ac3fa3d20f7d3c457587b` (531,456 bytes, exit 3), general protection `f0e9daacfa479945afec952692f69e3911b285e478f9ae8d12a3e14f0c091960` (531,456 bytes, exit 3), and contained user fault `795cb85aa599d2ead4e228bd0eb3da5ad28ecd8970955b294ab09f72c3f7ade7` (531,968 bytes, exit 0). Normal and contained-fault paths execute the canonical program in both generations, validate exact record-arena use, clean both typed aliases, reuse the root, exchange result `29`, and power off cleanly.

Exact implementation commit `f3eca7c8dab290e3916fbf33dcabc41d685a91bb` passes GitHub [Verify run 30753663882](https://github.com/eworker-inc/Windvale/actions/runs/30753663882). Windows and digest-pinned Debian 12 each pass all 67 Seed tests, all 25 OS tests, and the complete native CLI qualification gate. Seed elapsed time is 210.128 seconds on Windows and 210.185 seconds on Debian. Both logs emit the same 56 SHA-256 values in exact order, including the same canonical `Sum-Data.wvb` identity. This qualifies Probe 31 as the latest cross-host native/OS baseline and completes Phase 12's first same-module proof. QEMU execution remains Windows-only evidence.

This evidence does not qualify arbitrary WVB loading or verification, a dynamic namespace, independent resource lifetimes, general allocation, scheduling, JIT publication, broader traps, Hyper-V, physical hardware, or .NET retirement. A second real program, independently lived resource, or third runnable must choose the next boundary.

## Qualified Probe-32 second exact WVB

Qualified Decision 0103 advances admission to version 4, interpreter profile to 6, kernel memory to `WVKMEM09`, protected processes to `WVPROC11`, and firmware to Probe 32 while retaining ABI 17/context 7/service table 5, WVA seam 8, admission bridge 2, retained bridge 10, paging 4, `WVRES004`, and `WVBR002`.

The admitted resource is the exact 815-byte canonical WVB compiled from the existing cross-compiler fixture `Tests/Fixtures/Source-Wvb/Function-Only.wv`, SHA-256 `9ccfed0509e84bfc63979c6dc13170c14762efbdaa448b4c5894325f31aa7761`. Stage 0 and the Windvale-written compiler produce byte-identical bytes. Its four functions exercise `bool`, `u8`, `u32`, and `i32` constants and comparisons, local mutation, forward branches, and internal calls for exactly 199 guest instructions and result `6`. Profile 6 derives valid instruction boundaries before accepting branch targets.

The AOT interpreter consumes exactly 189,137 native instructions at dynamic depth 5; its largest frame is 1,900 slots under unchanged ABI 17's 2,048-slot cap. New verified-WVO call-graph preflight counts every reachable frame, return address, and the entry shim's saved `r15`, rejects recursion, and derives exactly 58,800 native stack bytes. Fifteen pages (61,440 bytes) are the smallest whole-page envelope; the former 13-page configuration page-faulted under pinned QEMU, and fourteen pages are arithmetically insufficient. The execution-scoped record arena is 1,024 bytes in the existing data page and consumes exactly 528 bytes for eleven 48-byte records; a measured 512-byte configuration exhausted after 480 bytes before the next record.

The local changed-files verifier completes a zero-warning Release build and all 67 Seed tests in 218.262 seconds. The focused Windows suite passes all 25 OS tests. Pinned Windows QEMU 11.0/Q35/TCG passes all four exact Probe-32 scenarios after the serialized process identity was corrected to `WVPROC11`: normal `a2c69181b55178b0e23c9b1012239a8fb1c8a53e2ffb089c8c59f45fa9dd0a6a` (714,752 bytes, exit 0), invalid opcode `04d801d25c0b5876fccb796d14b0c1ba14123f468200d5fb28728033867a5df5` (714,752 bytes, exit 3), general protection `b5abc555c11d3585b73e0d3e5391bbf33ca864933d8f0514265fce355273f799` (714,752 bytes, exit 3), and contained user fault `0233c10f323aaa3ee30dbe710b11c05f4d85a08fd88d6a52705c02f4b271347b` (715,264 bytes, exit 0).

Exact implementation commit `da938979ae9fe59e5f752bdb81359ded58a0e6ac` passes GitHub [Verify run 30758910402](https://github.com/eworker-inc/Windvale/actions/runs/30758910402). Windows and digest-pinned Debian 12 each pass all 67 Seed tests, all 25 OS tests, and the complete native CLI qualification gate. Seed elapsed time is 251.909 seconds on Windows and 200.120 seconds on Debian. This qualifies Decision 0103 and Probe 32 as the latest cross-host OS baseline. QEMU execution remains Windows-only evidence.

## Qualified ABI-18 typed block slots and WVKMEM10 compaction

Qualified Decision 0105 advances the backend to `x86-64-wvb-baseline-v18` while retaining WVB 1.6, WVO 1.0, execution context 7, service table 5, the ABI-16 64-parameter call convention, and the ABI-17 2,048-cell physical frame ceiling. Machine IR preserves globally canonical semantic value IDs and exact types, records a separate canonical physical map, and reuses exact-type ranges only across blocks whose operand stacks are independently verified empty. The selector reconstructs the map, rejects cross-block operands, and the fragment decoder retains descriptor alias/provenance checks across later cell redefinitions.

Exact compiler preflight clears the former slot-2,049 failure and reaches `WVN2003`: `Compilerˉcompileˉsourceˉwvb` uses the next unsupported native opcode, `Bytesˉfromˉu8`. For the retained Probe-32 interpreter, `Executeˉmain` keeps 1,236 semantic values but needs only 82 physical value cells and a 745-cell actual frame. The verified deepest call path consumes exactly 23,824 bytes, making six pages minimal. The deterministic interpreter WVO is 418,372 bytes, SHA-256 `c712a8cf7aa674b89e01e8fbc1632eeb6414fd1fde0bc4ead763e45c38037bb1`; the client uses 102 code pages, a 113-page reclaimable allocation, and a 134-page `WVKMEM10` arena. Probe 32's exact WVB, 199 guest instructions, result `6`, call depth, `WVPROC11` shape, paging, resource, and serial contracts remain unchanged.

Focused Windows verification passes all ten native compiler cases. The complete local Standard gate passes a zero-warning Release build, all 68 Seed tests including the 160.501-second golden contract, and all 25 OS tests; the complete Seed suite takes 216.323 seconds. Pinned Windows QEMU 11.0/Q35/TCG passes all four retained scenarios: normal `b8f0e656066b1e4f28edc4124eca6eea18130a0d6c0f4a9018e8ae817a0fa985` (531,456 bytes, exit 0), invalid opcode `0322ce3d3a9fecfa5c84809d8594f4f3ea643aaff2776f8d25668f1d723b9b54` (531,456 bytes, exit 3), general protection `1a0bd9f37c595d4170bd05fe83cc05dc344d2223674a01812f253ceb77893e40` (531,456 bytes, exit 3), and contained user fault `68319856b2913b3c857012d3fd38f147cf2a2307afacc9ffc8c8a33c005d0cf9` (531,968 bytes, exit 0).

Exact implementation commit `484c228c666e57bd6d3dc67d60c855f528cff3bf` passes GitHub [Verify run 30762156220](https://github.com/eworker-inc/Windvale/actions/runs/30762156220). Windows and digest-pinned Debian 12 each pass zero-warning Release builds, all 68 Seed tests, all 25 OS tests, and the complete native CLI qualification gate. Seed elapsed time is 237.931 seconds on Windows and 196.849 seconds on Debian; the golden contract takes 177.494 and 147.236 seconds respectively. This qualifies ABI 18, `WVKMEM10`, Decision 0105, and compacted Probe 32 as the latest cross-host native/OS baseline. QEMU execution remains Windows-only evidence.

## Qualified bounded straight-line WebAssembly backend

Decision 0106 extends target `wasm32-browser-v1-experimental` to a bounded one-function straight-line `i32` lowerer written in portable Windvale source. It accepts locals, constants, discard, checked addition/subtraction/multiplication/negation, and a final return while preserving execution ABI 1 result, `WVR3007`, and exact attempted-instruction evidence. The retained success artifact is 432 bytes with SHA-256 `15f2d58746ff2b0ae33a0de05e2781949c9d908fab46dd4072bfe3b2fa42b0bb`; the checked subtract, multiply, and negate overflow artifacts retain SHA-256 `757d26c2cf404cabcf5b78d2c998bc7ddc78ec4531e4571630ae2c1b5c8d7925`, `e924c7507a363a7b019935622abfbd4bf4ac8445cd37a0412130ce8e5c83d51a`, and `3f098efd63c68d8c62a4f6b373507e12c21808ff01120d165c9dc85a047e99e2`.

Exact implementation commit `a2285f5a0c09598ec701691bdbf0af9080e8cf0c` passes both host jobs in GitHub [Verify run 30762541741](https://github.com/eworker-inc/Windvale/actions/runs/30762541741). Windows and digest-pinned Debian 12 each complete a zero-warning Release build, all 68 Seed tests, all 25 OS tests, and the complete native CLI qualification gate. The WebAssembly test recompiles the Windvale backend and fixtures, requires exact backend/input/output digests, independently parses the emitted Wasm, compares status/result/instruction tuples with the reference runtime, and covers malformed and bounded failure cases without output.

The run-level conclusion is `cancelled` only because a later `main` documentation push activated `cancel-in-progress` after both host jobs had finished: Debian completed successfully at 19:09:53 UTC and Windows at 19:11:07 UTC. Their exact job conclusions remain `success`; no host qualification step failed or was cancelled. Node.js 24.18.0 separately validates and executes all six ABI artifacts on Windows. This qualifies deterministic Windows/Linux construction and differential semantics, not a permanent WebAssembly target.

## Local disposable-worker integration

Decision 0107 integrates the qualified `.wv` backend into the browser playground. The reusable engine recompiles the embedded core/tool composition once, requires backend WVB SHA-256 `b47a6f5b89ac0d58dc6cafd6489b1fb12f1a0b9b161c09e8d2ca5a438993076a`, preserves selector rejection without output, and reproduces the retained 432-byte Wasm SHA-256 `15f2d58746ff2b0ae33a0de05e2781949c9d908fab46dd4072bfe3b2fa42b0bb` from 301-byte WVB SHA-256 `f7d360cf4d717d2cce93eda4f2c814960c39f1dd04bd0f74c44f55066730d655`.

On 2026-08-02, the focused Seed playground test passed the exact backend, unsupported-fallback, deterministic-repeat, WVB, and Wasm assertions. A Release Blazor build completed with zero warnings or errors. A Chromium-based in-app browser then ran the `WebAssembly worker` example twice; each fresh worker validated the import-free module and returned ABI `1`, status `0`, result `42`, and 30 instructions equal to the .NET reference path. The browser console contained no warnings or errors.

Exact implementation commit `f3b96052b964832fb5fc60ed3d076b42e8b78e9d` passes GitHub [Verify run 30763774038](https://github.com/eworker-inc/Windvale/actions/runs/30763774038). Windows and digest-pinned Debian 12 each complete a zero-warning Release build, all 68 Seed tests, all 25 OS tests, and the complete native CLI gate. The cross-host Seed case recompiles the embedded `.wv` backend, reproduces the exact WVB/Wasm identities, verifies unsupported fallback without publication, and compares deterministic repeats. GitHub [Deploy homepage run 30763774003](https://github.com/eworker-inc/Windvale/actions/runs/30763774003) publishes the static playground from the same commit successfully. This qualifies the reusable engine and packaging integration; cross-browser qualification and worker containment for compilation, verification, lowering, and fallback execution remain open.

## Local standalone direct-artifact integration

Decision 0110 adds `/playground/wasm-demo/` as an ordinary HTML, CSS, and JavaScript route beside the editable playground. It embeds the exact qualified profile-3 bytes as deployment data, checks their 432-byte length and SHA-256 `15f2d58746ff2b0ae33a0de05e2781949c9d908fab46dd4072bfe3b2fa42b0bb`, and reuses Decision 0107's disposable worker. The route contains no Blazor bootstrap or .NET framework request; Stage 0 still owns artifact construction and qualification.

On 2026-08-02, `npm run verify:wasm-demo` reconstructs and validates the import-free artifact under Node.js and executes it twice with ABI `1`, status `0`, result `42`, and 30 instructions. A zero-warning Release build and publication retain the nested entry point and shared worker, and the focused Seed browser-playground engine case passes. A Chromium-based in-app browser runs the route to the same tuple with no warning or error; its seven observed static assets contain the page stylesheet, logo, analytics bootstrap, application module, artifact data, worker host, and worker, with no `_framework`, Blazor, or .NET asset. This local step does not by itself establish cross-browser or public-deployment qualification.

Exact implementation commit `e9480bca814318fb6fdcab0c4f3f1db699a01e6f` passes GitHub [Verify run 30767375189](https://github.com/eworker-inc/Windvale/actions/runs/30767375189), including the complete Windows and digest-pinned Debian 12 qualification jobs. GitHub [Deploy homepage run 30767375190](https://github.com/eworker-inc/Windvale/actions/runs/30767375190) passes the independent demo verifier and publishes successfully. The public `windvale.ca` route then reports the exact ABI tuple with no `_framework`, Blazor, or .NET asset and no browser warning. This qualifies the static packaging and deployed Chromium runtime boundary; cross-browser qualification remains open.

## Local bounded WebAssembly call-graph evidence

[Decision 0120](../Decisions/0120-Bounded-WebAssembly-Call-Graph.md) adds profile 6 for two through eight `i32`-returning functions, zero through two `i32` parameters, profile-3 scalar bodies, and direct calls only to a lower canonical ordinal. The order rule excludes recursion and cycles and statically caps dynamic depth at eight. The generated module uses real private WebAssembly functions behind the unchanged ABI-2 exports, with one shared instruction count, limit, and status across every callee.

The retained three-function source compiles to 399-byte WVB SHA-256 `502f5e9394248db4e21b49a3a98173917c2ff6f9a8252bef606a7a6c845d6482`. The deterministic 1,185-byte import-free Wasm SHA-256 is `d92667752762a992bdb626e34b83b78ee9c531f167b911737dfbf5f6443f3518`. The reference runtime, independent target decoder, and Node.js agree on `0/42/66`, one-instruction-short `3011/0/65`, and reset across a repeated successful run. A separate callee-overflow fixture produces 301-byte WVB SHA-256 `9e2b2a747287ff49ffce4d34f888b557a48064062e75ff5147bfc0224b54dca2` and 737-byte Wasm SHA-256 `4e936e5c4b077d1bce8719f5cc5c974961088f1171ed00158f9ac251f7652bd7`; overflow in `Calculate` propagates through `Main` as `3007/0/14`. Corrupt forward/self targets and arity mismatches fail as `Unsupportedˉcode` without output. The eight-function boundary is accepted and independently reconstructed, while nine functions fail without publication.

The standalone verifier reconstructs and executes the embedded artifact without a .NET asset. On 2026-08-02, a Chromium-based in-app browser loads the updated ordinary HTML page, runs both fresh workers, and reports the exact artifact identity, ABI `2`, both retained tuples, and zero .NET/Blazor requests. Focused Windows verification is green. Cross-host and cross-browser qualification remain pending.

## Local WebAssembly calls-with-control evidence

[Decision 0121](../Decisions/0121-WebAssembly-Calls-With-Structured-Control.md) composes the profile-6 call graph with profile-5 sequential structured control. The selector admits compiler-produced `bool` locals, independently reconstructs every region and call, and keeps one ABI-2 count, limit, and status across callers, callees, loop iterations, and conditional routes.

The true-route fixture compiles to 716-byte WVB SHA-256 `cf519c2d636d6e7b22b54afacf632bf5e514982e030d5c8b799a5585e7f39120` and deterministic 2,729-byte Wasm SHA-256 `3be50be3c2436638973eb68743f9fdd2e00df9816e50e498b432ff36468c3a77`. The false-route fixture produces 722-byte WVB SHA-256 `77e65ba692c8abc87dbac4dfeba174f3afc9191ac784b47a65becae8f0df2752` and 2,729-byte Wasm SHA-256 `35d75c30ef03dbb693a976cfaa31405ce90ecca4d393c5e93de8953fcf4658da`. The reference runtime, independent target decoder, and Node.js agree respectively on `0/42/196` plus `3011/0/195`, and `0/42/153` plus `3011/0/152`. An out-of-range branch target and a nested call/control program fail without publication. A fresh Chromium-based in-app browser reproduces `0/42/196` and `3011/0/195`, reports no warning or error, and observes no `.NET`, Blazor, or `_framework` asset. Cross-host and cross-browser qualification remain pending.

## Local WebAssembly linear-memory and UTF-8 evidence

[Decision 0123](../Decisions/0123-Versioned-WebAssembly-Linear-Memory-And-Utf8-Buffers.md) adds profile 8 and execution ABI 3 for the exact compiler-produced `bytes -> bytes` and `text -> text` identities. The emitted import-free modules expose one fixed, non-growable 129-page memory, a reserved first page, disjoint 4 MiB input and output regions, exact output kind and length, and the retained instruction counter. Text input is rejected by a generated strict UTF-8 state machine before WVB execution.

The bytes fixture compiles to 179-byte WVB SHA-256 `3d751ca734faed1832b4d33a9f0cfc605b695f3ae8156e3d431504798869c8d9` and deterministic 435-byte Wasm SHA-256 `b5f87bd47be7a0ce0bb6755de4ecea8bc311c9412ee28d6091092e7aa4c184f5`. The text fixture compiles to 178-byte WVB SHA-256 `c19463d24d65c1bc46dca48dcda8541491b53b7289483afe4508685f30e0fbda` and deterministic 791-byte Wasm SHA-256 `c3635b8df4ed9d471faad7e653e975662099c0a2336639586915ce50b768542d`.

The reference interpreter agrees on arbitrary bytes, Unicode text, and four charged WVB instructions. The independent target decoder reconstructs the exact WVB type and instruction shape plus Wasm types, fixed memory, ordered exports, globals, wrapper, meter, UTF-8 validator, and memory copy. Node.js round-trips empty, ordinary, Unicode, arbitrary-byte, and exact-4-MiB inputs; proves memory growth rejection; checks `3011/0/3` exhaustion and `3008/0/0` range failure; and compares curated malformed boundaries plus 20,000 deterministic generated byte strings with its independent fatal UTF-8 decoder. `npm run verify:wasm-demo` independently checks the embedded text artifact, memory contract, success, exhaustion, and malformed input without loading .NET. A Chromium-based in-app browser round-trips the default input and a 62-byte edited multilingual value with exact `0/4` success and `3011/3` exhaustion, zero .NET/Blazor requests, and no console warning or error. Cross-host and cross-browser qualification remain pending.

## Local WebAssembly runtime-value evidence

[Decision 0128](../Decisions/0128-Bounded-WebAssembly-Runtime-Values.md) adds profile 9 over unchanged execution ABI 3. The Windvale-authored selector now validates one straight-line `bytes -> bytes` function with `i32`, `bool`, `u8`, `u32`, and `bytes` locals; definite local initialization; a typed stack no deeper than four; byte lengths, slices, reads, widening, little-endian builders, concatenation, and one final return. Internal packed descriptors and a checked monotonic output arena remain private to generated code.

The representative fixture compiles to 914-byte WVB SHA-256 `6436f97c0e9abf131cc3a503c4449104706aa66eb0292a282a978fb7a5c5e100` and deterministic 4,878-byte Wasm SHA-256 `7bd5d2b0bc256503cd07dc300e528da38f8a09bcfec4c2b1007c1994db1b88f4`. The reference runtime and Node.js agree on its 19 output bytes and 155 charged instructions. Budget 154 returns `3011/154` with no output, while empty input reaches range status `3008` after 26 charged operations.

The focused 718-byte concatenation artifact SHA-256 `94533e9d01bdfcc606a3225ac28c774ecadd3cc0e0eccb02a7dba4f3fdb4ccb2` succeeds with an exact 4 MiB output and returns `3015/7` one input byte past the per-value boundary. The 797-byte u16 artifact SHA-256 `f312812fedae4c8dd45ffcb022301c1e85d7bdad4c71906a771cfc95333cde41` proves success, `3016/10` narrowing rejection, and `3008/7` range failure. The 921-byte arena artifact SHA-256 `0e37802a606ee67abd467ddc5da84f0d18807bb86b8bf497c4bdf0a41fa5a089` distinguishes aggregate `3018/14` from value-size `3015/14` at the input-capacity boundary.

The independent C# decoder verifies the source profile and completely consumes the emitted Wasm section, memory, global, ordered-export, local-type, opcode, meter, output-publication, and bulk-memory evidence. A not-yet-initialized local load is rejected without output. Node.js rebuilds and executes all four new artifacts, checks fixed-memory non-growth and invalid lengths before metering, and retains deterministic exact hashes. The focused Seed WebAssembly test and the 21-artifact WebAssembly verifier pass locally on Windows. Cross-host and browser execution evidence for profile 9 remain pending.

## Local Windvale-native WebAssembly WVB-envelope evidence

[Decision 0131](../Decisions/0131-Windvale-Native-WebAssembly-Wvb-Envelope-Verifier.md) adds profile 10 over unchanged execution ABI 3. The runtime-value selector admits checked unsigned addition and subtraction, unsigned comparisons, terminator-aligned jumps and branches, early bytes returns, and compiler-produced nested control. It lowers each validated WVB basic block through one private program-counter local and a single Wasm dispatch loop while preserving exact dynamic WVB instruction charging.

The first measured consumer is `Wvb-Envelope-Verify-Main.wv`. It compiles to 2,837-byte WVB SHA-256 `1362b2707a4ff442a1458e3f821e01108bb948858db21e022bfee05869c2fb86` and deterministic 14,902-byte import-free Wasm SHA-256 `f493777450b720ef786b60502528819969ad9e0322aa55a9c0259f6de20850fc`. The reference runtime and Node.js return `[1]` for its own canonical WVB after exactly 2,206 instructions; budget 2,205 returns `3011/2205` with empty output.

The companion checked-`u32` fixture has 447-byte WVB SHA-256 `d6ba02dfe12efdcb7c2f8ed6664551a776e79e3ff2c30134dc7e3642ee7ce743` and deterministic 1,893-byte Wasm SHA-256 `f645c0ff095eb06c825fea056659545cc258d857da55fc9dfd1a928812373f61`. It returns little-endian `42` after 57 instructions, `WVR3007` for addition overflow after 37 and subtraction underflow after 47, and `WVR3008` for a truncated read after seven.

Bad magic, an 11-byte truncated header, a hostile first-section length, and one trailing byte return `[0]` after 112, 92, 460, and 2,201 instructions. The independent C# decoder reconstructs the source opcode and target profile, terminator alignment, Wasm local layout, dispatch loop, conditional routes, branch depths, program-counter traffic, exact static meter count, output publication, fixed memory, ordered exports, and bulk-memory use. A branch target changed to point inside its own operand is rejected before output. The focused Seed WebAssembly test and the 23-artifact-plus-input Node.js verifier pass locally on Windows. Exact descendant `ea1aa89` passes Windows and Debian qualification including this portable envelope-verifier path; browser execution evidence for profile 10 remains pending.

## Local Windvale-native WebAssembly WVB-structural evidence

[Decision 0134](../Decisions/0134-Windvale-Native-WebAssembly-Wvb-Structural-Verifier.md) adds profile 11 over unchanged execution ABI 3. The single-runtime-function ceiling is now 2,047 nonparameter locals, 32,768 code bytes, and 100,000 decoded instructions; generated Wasm is bounded to 524,288 bytes. A one-pass byte mask makes decoded instruction boundaries constant-time control-target evidence, and the encoder accumulates one basic block before appending it to the complete function. The preexisting profile artifacts retain exact bytes.

`Wvb-Structural-Verify-Main.wv` compiles to 19,755-byte WVB SHA-256 `72da44ba1292ed3ef4ac62c239dd937862636229a7d60302305a7dd19ac27376`. Its function has 1,096 parameter-plus-local types, 4,062 instructions, and maximum stack two. The selector emits deterministic 113,385-byte import-free Wasm SHA-256 `46fe579fb7082dd4b0dd981e09f6b953127e52c9c6993d7885ca130725762677`. The reference runtime and Node.js return `[1]` for the verifier's own WVB after exactly 1,446,276 instructions; budget 1,446,275 returns `3011/1446275` with empty output.

The verifier completely consumes bounded module, capabilities, data, functions, code, exports, and types payloads. The 1,651-byte data/text, 1,781-byte nominal-types, and 849-byte hosted-capabilities fixtures return `[1]` after 103,696, 94,466, and 28,803 instructions. Invalid module profile, capability count 33, data count 4,097, function count 4,097, unknown code opcode, invalid export target, and truncated nonempty Types payload each return `[0]`.

The independent C# decoder completely consumes the large generated Wasm local and opcode stream and checks its exact meter, dispatch, output-publication, fixed-memory, and export evidence. The focused Seed WebAssembly test and the 24-artifact-plus-three-positive-input Node.js verifier pass locally on Windows. This is structural evidence only: UTF-8/name grammar, canonical ordering and identity, operand indices, type/local flow, control joins and reachability, declared stack agreement, and authorization remain for the semantic verifier. Cross-host and browser execution evidence for profile 11 remain pending.

## Local descriptor-bearing WebAssembly call evidence

[Decision 0139](../Decisions/0139-Descriptor-Bearing-WebAssembly-Call-Graph.md) adds profile 12 over unchanged execution ABI 3. The selector admits two through eight acyclic `bytes -> bytes` functions using the profile-11 runtime and terminator-aligned control operations. Each private Wasm function has target type `(i64) -> i64`; the existing packed descriptor remains private generated-code policy. Shared mutable globals carry status, instruction count, instruction limit, and the monotonic arena across every caller and callee while the exact ten-export public ABI remains unchanged.

`Runtime-Calls-Main.wv` compiles to 764-byte WVB SHA-256 `a44c8bdbf9983a7929a769d5ca2e0b60323d72cf96b04e31450d9757bb15729a`. Its three functions exercise nested calls, a call within conditional control, slicing, concatenation, and empty-input byte construction. The selector emits deterministic 4,086-byte import-free Wasm SHA-256 `5ee04d5b3b33399dce61709135709f0d0ebb7d6374e14759d83986859806eadd`. The reference runtime and Node.js both return `[9, 9, 8]` from `[9, 8]` in exactly 127 instructions; budget 126 returns `3011/126` with no output. Empty input returns `[0, 0]` in 63 instructions, and a repeated success proves wrapper and arena reset.

The independent C# decoder checks decreasing source call ordinals and consumes both Wasm types, all four function declarations, fixed memory, eleven globals, ordered exports, wrapper locals, and all private local layouts. A call operand changed to target its own caller is rejected without output. The focused Seed WebAssembly test passes, and the 25-artifact-plus-three-positive-input Node.js verifier rebuilds, validates, instantiates, and executes every output locally on Windows. The changed-scope Windows verifier also completes a zero-warning Release build and passes all 77 Seed tests, including the golden compiler contract; it is development feedback rather than conformance or cross-host qualification. Cross-host and browser execution evidence for profile 12 remain pending.

## Local modular WebAssembly WVB canonical metadata/reference evidence

[Decision 0144](../Decisions/0144-Modular-WebAssembly-Wvb-Canonical-Metadata-And-References.md) retains profile 12 and execution ABI 3 while adding the first eight-function WVB semantic phase. `Wvb-Semantic-Verify-Main.wv` separates identifier, structural, module/capability, data, function, code/export, type, and orchestration ownership. It validates canonical names and ordering, exact capability catalog signatures, strict text UTF-8, nominal declaration identities, local/function/capability/data/type operands, exact branch boundaries, export-to-function identity, record-to-enum field identity, and enum member/value uniqueness.

The source compiles to a 70,016-byte WVB with SHA-256 `09a665dcfbf8fe70d9b830be8376b1c1353a5ef09ff10de7b0183e535036fa64`. Its eight functions occupy exactly 65,536 aggregate code bytes. The hosted Windvale backend completes deterministic lowering in 129,151,253 instructions and emits 440,093-byte import-free Wasm SHA-256 `a2ef01881a4d381154a0e3feb0cb74cb0cdb3a53631cae1206d2fc03bcabe2fa`.

The reference runtime and Node.js return `[1]` for the data/text, nominal-type, and hosted-capability modules after exactly 1,122,085, 912,951, and 113,457 instructions. One fewer instruction on the data/text case produces `WVR3011` with empty output. Both engines return empty success output for thirteen structurally valid corruptions: module identifier, portable capability declaration, capability signature, data ordering, text UTF-8, function ordering, nominal function shape, text-constant data kind, branch boundary, export identity, cross-kind type identity, enum backing value, and record-to-enum field target. The Stage 0 oracle independently rejects every same byte sequence.

The independent C# decoder consumes the wrapper and eight private Wasm functions, both function types, fixed memory, eleven globals, exact ten-export ABI, and every private local layout. The focused Seed WebAssembly case and complete 26-artifact-plus-input Node.js gate pass locally on Windows. Changed-scope verification on base `dcc694a` produces a zero-warning Release build and passes all 80 selected Seed tests, including the golden compiler contract; it remains development feedback rather than conformance evidence. After rebase onto `c5e72bb`, the focused WebAssembly test passes again against the updated reference runtime. Allocation-free exact branch-boundary rescans are quadratic on branch-heavy input: the verifier's own unusually large WVB exceeds 500,000,000 verification instructions and is retained as optimization pressure rather than acceptance evidence.

This is not the complete mandatory semantic verifier. Typed operand-stack flow, type-correct access to deterministically default-valued locals, call value flow, record-field receiver identity, joins, reachability, maximum-stack agreement, authorization, cross-host construction, and browser execution evidence remain pending at Decision 0144.

[Decision 0146](../Decisions/0146-Expanded-Descriptor-Bearing-WebAssembly-Call-Graph.md) adds profile 13 over unchanged execution ABI 3 because the canonical semantic verifier reached exactly all eight functions and 65,536 aggregate code bytes in profile 12. The per-function local, code, instruction, stack, signature, control, and decreasing-call-ordinal rules remain unchanged. The bounded aggregate becomes sixteen functions, 131,072 code bytes, 400,000 decoded instructions, and 1,048,576 generated Wasm bytes; profile-12-sized inputs retain the 524,288-byte output ceiling.

A test-derived ninth semantic phase produces a 70,086-byte WVB with 65,567 aggregate code bytes and SHA-256 `1b88f7cdbaf6d00fcde3ac6dfa09f38ff721e90edf283018378e5004a91340b4`. It lowers in 130,307,230 instructions to 440,333 import-free Wasm bytes with SHA-256 `8601f90946c0738ad6ce20c236e219f66e4ee4b2b143242a430c203918b1259a`. The independent C# decoder consumes the wrapper plus nine private functions; a seventeen-function graph is rejected without output. Node.js agrees with the reference runtime on the data/text, nominal-type, and hosted-capability inputs at 1,122,092, 912,958, and 113,464 instructions and on one-instruction-short exhaustion.

The original 440,093-byte profile-12 semantic artifact remains byte-identical. The updated standalone selector core WVB is `14be02c61ddfa0b8bcfab5804de8edf7cefa4dffe8c8b0d24762aee63b728222`; the composed backend WVB is `fddf873732a2979c4a5fac25c02d10f91dcc71d7920b77293aed62354bb3e0f7`. Decision 0146 supplies capacity only; Decision 0149 subsequently consumes that capacity for executable type and compiler-aligned control flow.

[Decision 0149](../Decisions/0149-Windvale-Native-WebAssembly-Wvb-Executable-Verifier.md) adds a two-function portable phase after the retained seven semantic phases. `Hˉexecutable` proves typed local and operand-stack flow, primitive and bytes operations, function/capability arguments and results, record construction and receiver identity, enum identity, returns, and exact maximum stack. `Iˉcontrol` proves the source compiler's empty-stack boundary, terminator-target, and reachability contract. General WVB locals keep deterministic defaults; a load does not require an earlier store.

The two phase functions independently fit the retained profile-13 per-function limits: `Hˉexecutable` uses 2,019 locals, 32,766 code bytes, and maximum stack three; `Iˉcontrol` uses 564 locals, 9,793 code bytes, and maximum stack three. Their standalone WVB SHA-256 is `93fa740c433f291af082ce3e8d9fbd304d7e9547a30b6a59ac73b752eb68a02d`. The complete ten-function verifier is 115,483 WVB bytes with 108,331 aggregate code bytes and SHA-256 `6a26b09c0f96e3fa9edf8c180ee8f4b2551f1b1007f0faabcec39be1106285b4`.

The hosted Windvale backend lowers the complete verifier in 213,655,515 instructions to 722,837 import-free Wasm bytes with SHA-256 `6060b8198405b5f8763890ef5b53482398e1e0c7716f91ab279d9307db8d077b`. The Stage 0 playground adapter construction ceiling becomes 225,000,000 instructions. No execution ABI, fixed-memory extent, public export, or generated profile-12 artifact changes.

The reference runtime and Node.js return `[1]` for compiler-produced data/text, record/enum, and hosted-capability inputs. Node.js pins successful execution at 4,181,579; 3,250,582; and 241,607 instructions and returns `WVR3011` one instruction below the first budget. Nine structurally and canonically valid inputs pass the earlier semantic-only verifier but are rejected by both the Stage 0 oracle and the new executable verifier. Their Node.js counts are 1,489,699; 962,942; 972,877; 1,007,979; 1,018,861; 1,024,723; 1,586,834; 1,501,929; and 172,552 for operator kind, local store, call argument, record receiver, enum identity, branch condition, unreachable region, declared stack, and capability argument respectively.

The complete twenty-eight-artifact Node.js gate passes locally on Windows under Node.js 24.18.0. This establishes import-free executable-verifier behavior, not cross-host construction or cross-browser qualification. General nonempty stack joins, capability authorization, Wasm-hosted WVB execution, source compilation, and the default-playground switch remain pending.

## Local compiler-scale WebAssembly interpreter execution-entry evidence

[Decision 0175](../Decisions/0175-Compiler-Scale-Wasm-Interpreter-Execution-Entry.md) raises the retained interpreter to explicit compiler-scale bounds: 4,096 functions, 64 parameters, 2,048 combined parameter/local cells per function, stack depth 64, 400,000 aggregate instructions, a 20,000,000-instruction request ceiling, and call depth 64. Candidate-adaptive frames preserve a 128-cell minimum for small guests and round the candidate's maximum frame requirement to a bounded power of two. This prevents compiler capacity from imposing 2,048-cell frames on every retained workload.

The resulting interpreter is 67,251 WVB bytes / SHA-256 `cb6c3c8528a6da45c3f2cfd0c7faf63663b9015f9d58bd28996b789602dadd19`, with one function, 4,094 locals, 62,984 code bytes, 13,781 instructions, and maximum stack three. It lowers in 286,009,307 instructions to 412,367 import-free Wasm bytes / `f1ed2586544c92bce64cd45ae00ae44a34086f80fa0d08efc8ae8f6b5b3e7e47`.

The exact 597,545-byte portable compiler receives canonical WVSS for `Function-Only.wv`. With guest budget one and call depth 64, the reference runtime and Node.js agree: interpreter preflight completes, the compiler executes one instruction, and the versioned result is `WVXO 2` status `3011`, guest count one, and empty output after exactly 44,163,574 outer instructions. A separate request with the 20,000,000-instruction guest ceiling reaches enclosing status `3018`, emits no output, and consumes 44,978,597 outer instructions.

This moves the measured boundary from compiler shape to execution storage. The current interpreter reconstructs immutable byte-backed local frames on each store, while the generated Wasm uses one 4 MiB monotonic value arena; complete compiler execution therefore exhausts the enclosing arena before it can publish `WVCO 1`. Increasing that arena alone is not a durable fix because allocation follows local-store activity rather than live frame bytes. The next slice requires bounded reusable or reclaiming interpreter-owned local and saved-frame storage, or measured evidence that direct AOT is a smaller coherent route.

Every retained scalar, text, bytes, formatting, SHA-256, record, enum, resource-failure, and version-2 byte-entry case remains exact. The focused Seed WebAssembly case passes a zero-warning Release build in 104.588 test seconds. The complete 34-generated-Wasm-artifact gate rebuilds all artifacts, exercises both compiler adapters through the three verifier phases, runs the new execution-entry and arena-boundary cases, and passes under Node.js 24.18.0 on Windows in 424.7 seconds. Change-aware Windows verification then passes a zero-warning Release build and all 87 selected Seed tests in 495.977 suite seconds; the WebAssembly and golden cases take 104.832 and 255.095 seconds, and the complete command takes 507.5 seconds. Static-worker packaging, cross-host construction, Chromium/Firefox/WebKit execution, complete compiler execution, and the default-playground switch remain pending.

## Local exact per-function WebAssembly interpreter-frame evidence

[Decision 0177](../Decisions/0177-Exact-Per-Function-Wasm-Interpreter-Frames.md) replaces the candidate-wide power-of-two local-frame width with exact per-function frame lengths. Preflight records each function's parameter count and `(parameters + locals) * 8` byte length. Calls save only the return PC, exact caller locals, and caller function index; returns recover frame and code metadata from the verified tables. The declaration ceilings, WVB semantics, execution ABI 3, `WVXI`, `WVXO`, and complete-verifier boundary do not change.

The resulting interpreter is 67,209 WVB bytes / SHA-256 `b27b36959f0c8ac045d3fa653e728a1aa261a858dc0e4d7af4ff2aec527bd8d9`, with one function, 4,088 nonparameter locals, 62,948 code bytes, 13,769 instructions, and maximum stack three. The exact 597,545-byte portable compiler receives canonical `Function-Only.wv` WVSS at call depth 64. Guest budget one returns `WVXO 2` status `3011`, count one, and empty output after 44,171,762 outer instructions. The reference runtime and generated Wasm agree at budget 1,511 on `WVXO 2` status `3011`, count 1,511, empty output, and 49,727,640 outer instructions. In generated Wasm, budget 1,512 is the exact first enclosing `WVR3018`; it consumes 49,727,825 outer instructions and publishes no output. The 20,000,000 budget reaches that same outer count. The reference runtime retains its distinct, larger host allocation profile and is not expected to reproduce the target-specific 4 MiB failure.

Every retained positive and bounded-failure workload preserves its exact guest status, guest instruction count, and result under Node.js. The function/control workload succeeds and resets at 221,014 outer instructions, its one-short outer budget returns `3011` at 221,013, byte-array entry returns `[1, 2, 3, 42]` after 18,484, and the mutated typed-default and record-arena cases repeat at 68,358 and 2,019,332. The complete verifier's 523,386-instruction acceptance of the mutated default-local module remains unchanged.

The complete 34-generated-Wasm-artifact repository gate rebuilds the backend, verifiers, compilers, interpreter, and fixtures; rejects imports; validates every ABI; and passes under Node.js 24.18.0 on Windows in 446.1 seconds. Change-aware Windows verification then passes a zero-warning Release build and all 87 selected Seed tests in 472.695 suite seconds; its WebAssembly and golden cases take 106.091 and 230.761 seconds, and the complete command takes 491.5 seconds.

This is reduced immutable allocation, not reusable storage. `local.store` still rebuilds a byte sequence and the profile-16 value arena remains monotonic, so complete compiler execution and `WVCO 1` publication remain pending. Code inspection also excludes the existing direct selector as a shorter current route: it does not yet admit the compiler's static data, records, enums, recursion, or general verified call ordering. Bounded reclaiming/reusable interpreter storage remains the next measured implementation boundary before static-worker packaging and cross-browser qualification.

## Local bounded reclaiming WebAssembly value-storage evidence

[Decision 0189](../Decisions/0189-Bounded-Reclaiming-Wasm-Value-Storage.md) retains profile 16, execution ABI 3, fixed 129-page memory, and the exact ten public exports. The single-function runtime profile replaces monotonic constructed values with an address-ordered first-fit allocator over the unchanged 4 MiB output/value window. A fixed 49,188-byte metadata area holds 4,099 twelve-byte backing/length/reference entries; private Wasm helpers split free spans, retain slice and descriptor aliases, release final owners, insert by address, coalesce both adjacent neighbors, and reset all metadata before each public run.

`Runtime-Reclaim-Main.wv` compiles to 388 WVB bytes / SHA-256 `b08792268aa477086cd347a7ea01306ab530b187d81c71900c16e6a7af872e15`. Its 8,192 repeated concatenations cumulatively request 16 MiB through the fixed 4 MiB arena while retaining only the current value. The backend emits 2,399 deterministic import-free Wasm bytes / `5a89412a9f48e883a027da497406747f1c31c8eb0e6533f7103e52a078a8827a`. Node.js returns the exact doubled 2 KiB input after 262,167 instructions, returns `WVR3011` at 262,166, and repeats the exact success in the same instance. Exact success after both prior success and prior failure proves reset and reuse rather than capacity growth.

The interpreter preflight now builds compact immutable per-function offsets and eight-byte `(kind, nominal token)` cells for every parameter and local. Default-local shape lookup becomes constant time while malformed nominal indices remain rejected. The resulting interpreter is 66,312 WVB bytes / `d6cf7293f21e5fbdc80a92b356a6608b0a5f174ff873563eb2fc52c1c3fa5a90`, with 4,036 nonparameter locals, 62,103 code bytes, 13,588 instructions, and maximum stack three. It lowers in 309,791,380 instructions to 438,842 deterministic import-free Wasm bytes / `d80a665cc2e059450ac11b62dbc5dfa03fcab22bd5ed4fd9393520c9911dc0ba`.

The exact portable compiler now returns normal `WVXO 2` budget failures at guest counts 1,511 and 1,512 after 46,097,866 and 46,098,389 outer instructions. A 100,000-instruction guest request reaches guest instruction 37,085 and returns `WVXO 2` status `WVR3017` with no output after 63,171,965 outer instructions. This is the retained interpreter's separate 4 KiB guest-record arena, not generated-Wasm value storage. Near the former boundary, incremental interpretation falls from about 3,679 to about 610 outer instructions per guest instruction, an approximately 83% reduction.

The focused Seed WebAssembly case passes a zero-warning Release build and every exact artifact, semantic, malformed, reclamation, and 37,085-boundary assertion in 118.656 test seconds. The complete 35-generated-Wasm-artifact gate rebuilds all artifacts and passes under Node.js 24.18.0 on Windows in 447 seconds. After removing the temporary identity collector, the independent Node.js gate passes strictly over the exact rebuilt artifacts in 327.6 seconds. This is local development evidence. Guest record ownership/reclamation, the separately bounded guest heap, complete `WVCO 1` publication, worker packaging, cross-host construction, Chromium/Firefox/WebKit execution, and the default-playground switch remain pending.

## Local bounded reclaiming WebAssembly guest-record evidence

[Decision 0197](../Decisions/0197-Bounded-Reclaiming-Wasm-Guest-Records.md) retains the interpreter's 4 KiB guest-record arena while changing records from monotonic byte offsets to stable zero-through-511 field-cell handles. The 4,404-byte pressure fixture / `55675447848f249c911f848e45014d63916b51ea8c2c98ce5a26e150fe176f3c` first replaces one 16-field local 33 times, which succeeds only with reclamation. The independent verifier recovers the exact native bytes and `Main` offset and rejects truncated, oversized, trailing, noncanonical, and targeted corruptions across DOS, COFF, optional-header, section, startup, context, padding, and relocation boundaries. The complete local Windows qualification command finishes in 549.4 seconds. ## Implemented ABI-21 frame-owned direct records

[Decision 0133](../Decisions/0133-Frame-Owned-Direct-Native-Records.md) consumes the exact Decision 0118 maps in the single shared selector used by host execution, both version-1 console containers, and Windvale OS. Direct record fields occupy deterministic persistent or block-reused frame backing; handles carry backing pointers; internal calls pass those pointers; and returns copy into caller-owned destinations. Assigned parameters receive owned copies before ordinary operations. Record-valued fields remain rejected.

The independent fragment verifier decodes nominal tags, complete field copies, frame ranges, local ownership copies, borrowed record references, caller-owned destinations, direct-field returns, and record call-graph agreement. Caller and callee stack decoding tests the complete 32-byte borrowed-descriptor form before the shared 16-byte record-pointer prefix. Corrupt planned construction backing fails as `WVN3030`. A 131,072-construction loop returns `131071` with zero record-arena use and the nominal interpreter/JIT/WVO-AOT result remains `42`.

The exact 328-function compiler retains map digest `aff287fba46a840e454e4cc7bf4751d3152474caf09331a526f3730ba280816e` and the 1,489-cell maximum. Its ABI-21 fragment is 16,905,513 bytes with SHA-256 `29a8b354e185fad4b4d8967ee8e263ce68cb9939373d91fb1e7919be887c8569`. The native compiler emits the exact canonical 815-byte function-only module and existing success report with no diagnostics, consumes 4,340,388 dynamic text/byte bytes, and consumes zero record-arena bytes. The full 12-module bootstrap clears `WVR3017` and deterministically reaches the retained 16 MiB dynamic-value boundary as `WVR3018` before output.

The measured explicit-copy fragment requires a synchronized 32 MiB code ceiling below the retained 34 MiB final-image ceiling. The 7,189-byte Windvale publication core has SHA-256 `f2c315c4c52099b8682396358563eef2eb9dceecf1feb84ce5bef5f8465bdeba`; the regenerated 7,105-byte retained bridge has SHA-256 `b21e1136fc9087f530391127a1e1400e7248fa1831a51f00d86d467cf5133cb0`. Stage 0 and Windvale planners accept exactly 32 MiB and reject 32 MiB plus one.

The paired Windows and Linux console targets rebuild through the same ABI-21 preparation boundary rather than retaining ABI 20. ABI 21 leaves scalar native selection unchanged; the integrated WVA startup templates produce the canonical 5,120-byte PE / SHA-256 `5947c00a81f4cf94651d42d619f3173a622448d042f4fa20e3042940d4a56c77` and 8,304-byte ELF / SHA-256 `8af8b46c290965cfc4475d882ac2d5fbdb0ffe4c493a19883a19c2683a319ec4`. Nominal-record container execution consumes zero bytes from their retained compatibility record arena.

The shared OS rebuild also consumes the same projected frame plan rather than retaining ABI 20. Probe 32's `Executeˉmain` grows from 745 to 755 frame cells and its independently measured deepest call path from 23,824 to 24,240 bytes, still inside the minimal six-page stack. The interpreter WVO is 445,684 bytes / SHA-256 `3840f10bacf8b7b498f28646b947a53841baf00241cd21bc94423ab5a43e8e31`; the normal linked client is 445,085 bytes / `95a80e8998571dfb1cb8af7e2d14fd40804f648ce73e136e7aa00265934d7f30`. The client needs 109 RX pages and a 120-page root, so `WVKMEM11` supplies a 141-page arena. Its retained 1,024-byte context record arena ends at exactly zero bytes used instead of 528.

Immediately before the Decision 0131 WebAssembly and Decision 0132 console-verification rebases, the integrated native/console/OS candidate completed Windows Qualification in 518.6 seconds. It produced a zero-warning Release build, passed all 77 Seed tests in 262.591 seconds including the 179.808-second golden compiler contract, passed all 31 OS tests, and completed the native CLI gate. Decision 0131 changes no native, console, or OS implementation/artifact input. After its rebase, focused WebAssembly, console, native-record, and all 31 OS checks passed. Decision 0132 retains the exact console bytes and changes no native or OS input; after its rebase, all four console checks and the native-record check pass. All four pinned Windows QEMU 11.0/Q35/TCG scenarios pass with exact rebuilt identities: normal `d394eb13ae93b71450bb9a3fd42e3dee0aaa17c58c6c920f031eacaf9deb5e8d` (555,520 bytes, exit 0), invalid opcode `ad77085ef6a9dbbd56dd938c3492748f363fb99c5719341387a6e87a75624ade` (555,520 bytes, exit 3), general protection `3a651d555f2c334c53b67385ac1fb731a513deb058e742ba5cbc58a985970de2` (555,520 bytes, exit 3), and contained user fault `198e01840c888d344e40ef985c574e33c77cdafccf36cdb56f9f9218bf74e8ca` (556,032 bytes, exit 0).

Exact commit `ea1aa89ba204ead633f8340c61b2bacc716881fd` passes GitHub [Verify run 30783457203](https://github.com/eworker-inc/Windvale/actions/runs/30783457203). Windows and digest-pinned Debian 12 each complete a zero-warning Release build, all 77 Seed tests, all 31 OS tests, the golden compiler contract, and the native CLI gate. ## Local exact-compiler dynamic-value profile

[Decision 0136](../Decisions/0136-Exact-Compiler-Dynamic-Value-Pressure.md) adds an opt-in reference-runtime and CLI profile for every allocation-bearing operation admitted by ABI 21. It reports constructed values and their flat result bytes by function and class without changing default runtime allocation, output, bytecode, or native execution.

The canonical 12-module Stage 1 run succeeds in exactly 6,700,562,174 instructions and reproduces the exact 599,868-byte Stage 2 compiler with SHA-256 `9673bf3331763181f443ec67b7a513bc66daa718969f7f6b0d197a4186071066`. It constructs 1,852,773 profiled values representing 902,262,268 flat bytes. `bytes.concat` contributes 962,611 values and 899,106,127 bytes, approximately 99.65% of the total; the next largest class is `bytes.from_u32_little` at 3,020,804 bytes.

The leading row is `Compilerˉsourceˉwirˉmergeˉfunction`: five escaping concatenated payloads per successful merge accumulate 315,298,984 flat bytes across 1,640 values. `Compilerˉsourceˉwirˉemit` adds 265,306,656 bytes across 451,269 concatenations, and `Compilerˉcompileˉsourceˉwvb` adds 102,543,288 bytes across 671 concatenations. This rules out a fixed capacity increase and shows why callee-only reset is insufficient, while not yet claiming a peak-live-byte count or selecting ownership, chunking, or collection.

The focused profiler test passes after a zero-warning Release build in 0.521 seconds. The successful full-bootstrap profile completes locally on Windows in 426.148 seconds. Cross-host qualification is pending; no OS input changed, so QEMU is not rerun for this diagnostic-only slice.

## Local exact-compiler dynamic-value lifetime

[Decision 0141](../Decisions/0141-Exact-Compiler-Dynamic-Value-Lifetime.md) follows each conceptual flat backing through verified operand stacks, local frames, calls, returns, borrowed byte/text views, and direct records. It counts unique reachable backing storage and separately retains popped input plus new-output overlap at allocation operations. The profiler is opt-in and leaves default execution unchanged.

The canonical 12-module Stage 1 run again returns zero after exactly 6,700,562,174 instructions and reproduces the exact 599,868-byte Stage 2 compiler with SHA-256 `9673bf3331763181f443ec67b7a513bc66daa718969f7f6b0d197a4186071066`. Decision 0136's 1,852,773 constructed values and 902,262,268 constructed bytes reduce to a 17-backing, 9,030,829-byte ideal live peak. The allocation-operation peak is identical and occurs at `Compilerˉsourceˉwirˉdirectory` / `bytes.concat`; retained backings and bytes are both zero after completion.

The peak leaves 7,746,387 bytes, approximately 7.39 MiB, inside the unchanged 16 MiB native arena before metadata and fragmentation. Two local Windows Release executions reproduce the exact result and artifact identity in 512.450 and 517.510 seconds. The bounded Foundation CLI fixture deterministically reports 8,388,672 constructed bytes, a 6,291,475-byte/five-backing peak, and zero retained roots. The focused dynamic tests pass after a zero-warning build; QEMU is not rerun because this diagnostic slice changes no OS input or artifact.

After linear integration of Decisions 0137 through 0139, change-aware Windows verification completes a zero-warning Release build and passes all 80 selected Seed tests in 314.943 suite seconds. The exact golden compiler contract takes 219.181 seconds and the warm lifetime case takes three milliseconds. The new WVB 1.7 and WebAssembly paths do not execute in the canonical WVB 1.6 lifetime profile, so the separate multi-billion-instruction measurement is not repeated after that additive integration.

Exact implementation commit `f6952a1b2cf18222de77b0260060dcff3a9ed4bc` passes GitHub [Verify run 30788097110](https://github.com/eworker-inc/Windvale/actions/runs/30788097110). The complete Windows and pinned-Debian verifier jobs both pass, including the deterministic bounded lifetime report. This cross-host qualifies the support and ordinary fixture; the multi-billion-instruction exact compiler profile remains Windows-local evidence.

## Local bounded exact-compiler allocator replay

[Decision 0143](../Decisions/0143-Bounded-First-Fit-Dynamic-Arena-Replay.md) replays the same typed lifetime trace through one fixed 16,777,216-byte arena. Each backing receives a 16-byte in-band header and the complete block is aligned to 16 bytes. Address-ordered first fit splits the low end of the first sufficient span, and release coalesces adjacent spans immediately. Allocating-operation inputs remain occupied until their result is placed. The opt-in diagnostic policy leaves ABI 21 and ordinary reference execution unchanged.

The canonical Stage 1 compiler again returns zero after exactly 6,700,562,174 instructions and reproduces the exact 599,868-byte Stage 2 compiler with SHA-256 `9673bf3331763181f443ec67b7a513bc66daa718969f7f6b0d197a4186071066`. All 1,852,773 allocations succeed and 1,852,722 reuse previously addressed storage. Peak payload is 9,030,829 bytes; headers and alignment raise peak charge by 387 bytes to 9,031,216 across 17 blocks. First-fit reaches address 10,700,368, leaving 6,076,848 bytes above its maximum extent. External fragmentation peaks at 7,324,224 bytes across at most 86 free spans. Completion retains zero blocks and charged bytes and restores one fully coalesced arena. The Windows Release replay takes 622.891 seconds.

The bounded Foundation CLI fixture performs 35 allocations, reuses 12, peaks at 6,291,600 charged bytes across five blocks, reaches address 8,389,040, and retains nothing. A focused 64-byte fixture proves split, adjacent coalescing, and reuse for a later 64-byte request. At 48 bytes it admits the first 32-byte block, rejects the next against a 16-byte largest span as `WVR3018`, and still recovers all storage; a 63-byte misaligned arena is rejected before execution. No WVB/WVO bytes, source semantics, native ABI, generated machine bytes, OS source, or guest artifact changes, so QEMU is not rerun.

Change-aware Windows verification completes a zero-warning Release build and passes all 81 selected Seed tests in 350.412 suite seconds. The golden compiler contract takes 246.518 seconds and the allocator/coalescing case takes four milliseconds. This is proportional local development evidence rather than dual-host qualification.

Exact implementation commit `c5e72bb12f6c7d1ec54033d299403a1128eb1bef` passes GitHub [Verify run 30790280704](https://github.com/eworker-inc/Windvale/actions/runs/30790280704). Windows and digest-pinned Debian 12 both pass the complete repository verifier, including the bounded allocator fixture. This qualifies the replay support and ordinary fixture across both hosts; the exact multi-billion-instruction replay remains Windows-local evidence.

## Local exact-compiler native descriptor ownership plan

[Decision 0147](../Decisions/0147-Native-Descriptor-Ownership-Plan.md) attaches a bounded version-1 ownership plan to native machine IR and verifies it through a separately implemented reconstruction before x86-64 selection. The action plan distinguishes static and hosted borrows, allocations, aliases, local and direct-record replacement, last-use release, call borrowing, caller result acceptance, callee transfer, and normal-return frame cleanup. Terminal native failure retains the ABI-21 whole-arena teardown policy.

The exact 328-function compiler produces 186,557 deterministic actions: 435 acquisitions, 172 static or hosted borrows, 34,772 retains, 144,983 releases, 3,546 call borrows, 1,660 accepted returns, and 989 transferred returns. Its carriers cover 293 descriptor parameters, 3,190 descriptor locals, 524 descriptor-bearing record-parameter fields, 9,287 descriptor-bearing record-local fields, 6,182 descriptor values, and 17,898 descriptor-bearing record-value fields. Source parameters are immutable, so assigned parameter counts are zero. The largest function is index 204, `Compilerˉsourceˉwirˉcompileˉblock`, with 48,242 actions. The complete action-map SHA-256 is `8681cfd9d8c96e3d5dc70c2b97f62795c2e29b632fb66065f2dea8ca102b0511`.

A focused fixture covers allocation, static borrowing, slicing, mutable descriptor replacement, two descriptor-bearing direct-record fields, call borrowing, record/descriptor returns, and field selection. Repeated lowering is identical, independent reconstruction agrees, the unchanged ABI-21 fragment executes to result zero, and corrupt action/count plans fail as `WVN2903`. The exact compiler plan contains both static and host-snapshot borrows. ABI 21, WVB/WVO, native fragments, host containers, OS inputs, and QEMU artifacts remain unchanged; native execution still reaches `WVR3018` until a successor ABI consumes the plan.

After rebase onto the modular WebAssembly verifier and capability-bearing platform-library candidates, focused ownership and exact-compiler checks pass again. Change-aware Windows verification completes a zero-warning Release build and passes all 83 selected Seed tests in 324.221 suite seconds; the golden compiler contract takes 213.423 seconds. Following the later Decision 0146 WebAssembly call-graph rebase, the focused ownership and exact-compiler checks pass once more with a zero-warning build. This is development feedback over the integrated tree, not dual-host qualification.

## Local WVA native descriptor allocator leaf

[Decision 0148](../Decisions/0148-First-Wva-Native-Descriptor-Allocator-Leaf.md) implements the Decision 0143 arena policy as one platform-neutral WVA x86-64 leaf while keeping it outside ABI-21 selection. A version-1 32-byte state owns the arena pointer, capacity, free-list head, allocated-block count, and charged-byte count. A version-1 40-byte request selects acquire, retain, or release and returns a closed status, owner token, data pointer, and exact charge. Token zero remains borrowed; an allocated token is its 16-byte header offset plus one.

The canonical 3,087-byte WVO has SHA-256 `75d82e97fcc6652b0153ebed1b849569248ca4371c3c365605f32092a17f4cfb`. Its one relocation-free 2,989-byte `.text` export has SHA-256 `67a8b6648389589b59ca1dd6b6b87e80fafaa31696d496e14be9fcf4711ccf70`. Stage 0 reassembles the checked-in WVA to those exact bytes, the bounded loader rejects changed code, and live writable-then-executable publication invokes the same code on the host.

After every live operation, an independently implemented C# reference model agrees on the complete request, allocator state, and 128-byte arena. The sequence covers four allocations and exhaustion; retain and non-final release; plain ordered insertion; predecessor, successor, and two-sided coalescing; full-arena reuse; stale-owner rejection; borrowed no-ops; reference-count overflow; and a corrupt free-list link. The end state restores one full free span.

The exact Decision 0147 plan projects its 435 acquisitions, 34,772 retains, and 144,983 releases to 180,190 allocator leaf calls. The remaining 6,367 static/host borrows, call borrows, accepted returns, and transferred returns move ownership without changing a reference count. Focused allocator, ownership, and exact-compiler boundary checks pass after zero-warning Release builds. Change-aware Windows verification completes another zero-warning build and passes all 84 selected Seed tests in 343.962 suite seconds; the golden compiler contract takes 215.835 seconds and the allocator case takes eight milliseconds. ABI 21, context 7, service table 5, WVB/WVO formats, selected machine fragments, host containers, OS inputs, and QEMU artifacts remain unchanged.

Exact commit `85af9c502467b236877fc77dcdac344c56b09735` passes GitHub [Verify run 30795423694](https://github.com/eworker-inc/Windvale/actions/runs/30795423694). Windows and digest-pinned Debian 12 each complete zero-warning builds and all 84 selected Seed tests. Windows takes 319.682 suite seconds with a 201.809-second golden contract and nine-millisecond allocator case. Debian takes 250.651 suite seconds with a 157.529-second golden contract and six-millisecond allocator case. This cross-host qualifies the canonical WVA/WVO identity, platform-common live calling convention, independent reference agreement, and integrated loader behavior.
