## Local Windvale directory-service IPC

[Decision 0154](../Decisions/0154-First-Windvale-Directory-Service-Ipc.md) defines a 28-through-283-byte `WVDQ 1` request and reuses the exact 24-through-3,096-byte `WVDR 1` response. The endpoint selects one pre-bound immutable snapshot. No path, handle, provider identity, request wrapper, or filesystem parsing enters the kernel. Structurally malformed requests and provider responses publish no reply; representable invalid names and limits retain the typed application outcomes without invoking the provider.

Portable `Directory-Service-Core.wv` owns request parsing, semantic validation, local rejection, and independent response checking. The hosted bridge declares only `file.read_bytes` and `filesystem.directory_read_v1`; its output agrees byte for byte with the independently implemented Stage 0 codec/provider oracle. The prior resource-named exchange implementation is extracted into one shared format-blind 4 KiB copied-message/lifecycle oracle used by both protocols.

After a zero-warning Release build, all 34 focused OS tests pass in 13.1 seconds. The new evidence covers the 283-byte request edge, maximal 3,096-byte response, middle/end/beyond-end reads, missing and non-file objects, invalid-name/limit non-invocation, malformed no-reply behavior, provider corruption, 512 deterministic hostile inputs, peer-exit cleanup, authorization and missing input, deterministic WVB reconstruction, and Windvale/Stage 0 byte equality. The portable core is 8,389 bytes / SHA-256 `7433ffde4399862eb2cdf46c0ea43d8d39fc7aec56b2182d6e7789bcb29b2179`; the hosted bridge is 8,492 bytes / `465b66c8fd21683c33cb9157fa13830c655147a36af50cc0961331fb5967ba2a`.

This is local protocol evidence. Probe 34 and every guest/firmware identity remain unchanged. Live adoption requires a dedicated page-sized reply window, a separate init-owned immutable directory snapshot, a checked runtime adapter, versioned process/memory/resource evidence, and QEMU execution; none is claimed here.

## Local immutable Windvale directory snapshot

[Decision 0155](../Decisions/0155-First-Immutable-Windvale-Directory-Snapshot.md) defines the separate init/service provider value required by Decision 0154. `WVDS 1` is a one-through-64-entry, one-page immutable format with fixed 32-byte header and entry records, strict ordinal ASCII segments, exact name/data packing, zero alignment, and no trailing bytes. It is deliberately distinct from the package-oriented `WVRS 1` store and remains opaque to the kernel.

Portable `Directory-Snapshot.wv` owns complete verification, lookup, and exact `WVDR 1` construction. `Directory-Snapshot-Service.wv` composes it with the provider-independent directory protocol rather than making `Directory-Service-Core.wv` depend on one storage format. The hosted bridge declares only `file.read_bytes`; its maximal 3,096-byte output agrees byte for byte with the independently implemented Stage 0 writer/verifier/provider oracle.

After a zero-warning Release build, all 37 focused OS tests pass. The three new cases cover input-order-independent construction, exact regions and coverage, the 3,184-byte page fixture, maximal success, missing/non-file/invalid-offset outcomes, malformed headers/entries/names/extents/padding, writer rejection, 256 deterministic hostile inputs, missing and unauthorized inputs, malformed no-reply behavior, repeated WVB construction, and Windvale/Stage 0 byte equality.

The canonical snapshot has SHA-256 `0f793a41a701240b9cf41179dafa252384b43cd23214646ff021d245657c235a`. Deterministic WVB identities are: 12,121-byte snapshot module / `7741b8a4005aff12276d3e151b2e991192d132585ceb758f8155d6eab098a137`; 20,061-byte composed service / `fc77da4c957dd7c44087012c3f911124b7904ec968ef3b21fc768ca0d6078316`; and 20,294-byte hosted bridge / `3f45fffcc9aee26fec35661c8a4ecf1b7b27e4f0a3fb0f0027fed0a78580fa4b`.

This is local format/service evidence. Probe 34 and all guest/firmware identities remain unchanged. Cross-host verification and guest adoption into new process, memory, resource, and QEMU identities remain pending.

## Qualified ABI-22 bounded dynamic-value lifetimes

[Decision 0150](../Decisions/0150-Bounded-Native-Dynamic-Value-Lifetimes.md) advances the single shared selector and independent decoder to ABI 22. Generated byte buffers of at least 64 bytes carry an eight-byte checked capacity/generation header; `Bytesˉconcat` reuses only a valid current owner at the arena tail, doubles capacity through 2 MiB, and caps it at 4 MiB. Complete descriptor copies propagate the high word. Every non-entry descriptor/record hidden result saves the entry arena cursor; direct descriptor returns reset or compact safe ranges, and scalar-only record returns roll back. Descriptor-bearing aggregate relocation remains deferred until caller-liveness evidence makes it safe. Corrupt ownership thresholds and checkpoint stencils fail as `WVN3030`.

The integrated Probe-34 rebuild retains its 755-cell `Executeˉmain`, 24,240-byte deepest call path, six client stack pages, zero record-arena use, exact guest WVB, `WVPROC13`, dynamic resource lookup, and terminal peer cleanup. Its interpreter WVO remains 447,652 bytes / SHA-256 `0748200721cab7d5c3c6a43916fc623dfa0ee35e304fea6ad899877c9601c8e2`; the normal linked client becomes 447,757 bytes / `aa3fdd6e836c71add4f24b6992fcfae090bf8d5aa056ec068a9c21dea516c919`, and the fault client becomes 447,741 bytes / `3344865517e56066ec0b8fbd7e5f80695a715fa04adc9959a876a73e04bbbbe8`. The client needs 110 RX pages and a 121-page root, so `WVKMEM13` supplies a 144-page arena beside the retained 11-page init/resource extent. ## Qualified bounded exact-compiler publication

[Decision 0111](../Decisions/0111-Bounded-Exact-Compiler-Fragment-Publication.md) attributes the exact compiler's 4,556,121 selected bytes before revising a security boundary. Function code accounts for 4,555,263 bytes across 328 functions and 191,632 machine-IR operations; alignment and immutable data account for only 858 bytes. The 48,578 zeroed frame slots currently emit 1,360,840 bytes, so eliminating all frame initialization would still leave 3,195,281 bytes. This rules out a local encoding change as an honest way to retain the 1 MiB whole-fragment ceiling.

The implemented hard fragment limit is 8 MiB, below the retained 34 MiB publication-image ceiling. ABI 20, target bytes, WVB, WVO, context 7, and service table 5 do not change. The exact compiler code remains 4,556,121 bytes with SHA-256 `8e74707df03a535e3ef68cfcfc8da6fa68fda29ccf4344e272fc50c8a5845bab`, passes the independent fragment decoder, and enters live W^X publication. Execution against a real source file then deterministically reaches `WVR3017` at the retained 1 MiB immutable-record arena before stdout, diagnostics, or output publication. The separate 4 MiB WVO/object and flat-linker limits remain unchanged.

The Windvale-written publication planner retains `WVPQ 1`, `WVPL 1`, canonical placement, and the 34 MiB image limit while accepting exactly 8 MiB and rejecting 8 MiB plus one. Its 7,189-byte portable core has SHA-256 `19e111490cba6f3dcae963169be82c8033d267ea505c30850502ae36fb36e13c`; its regenerated 7,105-byte retained bridge has SHA-256 `5ad896d92368dcadc61f358d51f5786408d9f1dc977efa5f522f99230f3ed51e`. An 8 MiB plus one fragment fails independently as `WVN3005`.

Windows Development passes a zero-warning Release build, all 67 regular Seed tests, and all 25 OS tests in 67.4 seconds wall time. Windows Standard passes all 68 Seed tests and all 25 OS tests in 219.6 seconds wall time. The Seed suite takes 206.326 seconds, including 153.641 seconds for the qualification-only golden compiler contract.

Exact implementation commit `e139e4e391192c115a1cabd92a1e0ab3bd71d31d` passes GitHub [Verify run 30768107059](https://github.com/eworker-inc/Windvale/actions/runs/30768107059). Windows and digest-pinned Debian 12 each complete a zero-warning Release build, all 68 Seed tests, all 25 OS tests, exact retained-planner reproduction, and the complete native CLI gate. Windows Seed takes 227.915 seconds with a 164.428-second golden contract; Linux Seed takes 207.347 seconds with a 153.987-second golden contract. The complete jobs finish in 8m29s and 7m38s respectively. QEMU was not rerun because ABI 20, every generated machine byte, and all OS source/artifact inputs remain unchanged.

## Qualified ABI-20 two-byte construction

Qualified Decision 0109 advances the implementation to `x86-64-wvb-baseline-v20` while retaining WVB 1.6, WVO 1.0, execution context 7, service table 5, the 64-parameter convention, ABI 18's canonical typed block-slot map, and the 2,048-cell physical frame ceiling. Verified `Bytesˉfromˉu16ˉlittle` now lowers from `U32` to a two-byte execution-arena-backed descriptor only after an unsigned 65,535 upper-bound proof. Detail 12 maps a larger value through packed status 5 to `WVR3016` before arena mutation.

The focused borrowed-bytes test passes on Windows. It executes valid values zero and 65,535 through the reference interpreter, W^X JIT, and linked WVO/AOT, checks the first invalid value 65,536 under interpreter and both native publication forms, reproduces deterministic fragment and WVO bytes, and rejects corrupt range constants/branches, allocation/store widths, failure details, and scalar/result aliases. Exact compiler preflight clears the last observed unsupported operation, completes x86-64 selection, and reports `WVN2902`: 4,556,121 selected bytes against the retained 1,048,576-byte fragment limit.

The Windows Development gate passes a zero-warning Release build, all 67 regular Seed tests, and all 25 OS tests. The complete Standard gate passes all 68 Seed tests, including the golden compiler-reproduction contract, and all 25 OS tests in 215.085 seconds. All four pinned Windows QEMU 11.0/Q35/TCG scenarios pass with the exact retained Probe-32 identities: normal `b8f0e656066b1e4f28edc4124eca6eea18130a0d6c0f4a9018e8ae817a0fa985`, invalid opcode `0322ce3d3a9fecfa5c84809d8594f4f3ea643aaff2776f8d25668f1d723b9b54`, general protection `1a0bd9f37c595d4170bd05fe83cc05dc344d2223674a01812f253ceb77893e40`, and contained user fault `68319856b2913b3c857012d3fd38f147cf2a2307afacc9ffc8c8a33c005d0cf9`.

Exact implementation commit `a63ca0fe1d9d66882ead68ed28bcd7cd6a067f23` passes GitHub [Verify run 30766123518](https://github.com/eworker-inc/Windvale/actions/runs/30766123518). Windows and digest-pinned Debian 12 each pass a zero-warning Release build, all 68 Seed tests, all 25 OS tests, and the complete native CLI gate. Seed elapsed time is 247.807 seconds on Windows and 202.369 seconds on Debian; the golden contract takes 188.544 and 150.427 seconds respectively. This qualifies ABI 20 and Decision 0109 as the latest shared native/OS baseline while retaining Probe 32's exact firmware identities. QEMU execution remains Windows-only evidence.
## Prior Stage 0 linker qualification (WVB 1.5)

Commit `9c4b9f5` was archived and transferred with matching SHA-256 `44565d0f6366eb418dc1a4555d1f7b355df63ec2ea836440de54639087fc335a`, then verified in the uniquely named disposable directory `/tmp/windvale-linker-9c4b9f5-20260730` on the isolated E-Worker QA host. The archive contained the canonical WVA fixture as 403 bytes plus the linker project, provider fixture, and shell verifier. The host ran Debian GNU/Linux 12 x64 with .NET SDK `10.0.302`. The verification did not use E-Worker release, configuration, service, or durable-data paths. The Debian report was retrieved with SHA-256 `b84cd3a180e8cb167dca3cf909457de91a8643f19911628c426c6e52c6fe5fc0`; the provider WVO, linked image, and canonical map were retrieved separately and each matched the Windows artifact byte for byte; then the resolved exact temporary directory and transferred archive were removed.

An initial QA attempt at `57f2544` exposed that `.wva` lacked an explicit archive line-ending attribute: the Windows working file was LF while the exported Debian fixture was CRLF, producing semantically equal scans but different byte-count reports. Commit `e5fd109` added `*.wva text eol=lf`; its archive lists the canonical fixture as 403 bytes, and the complete qualification was rerun from that replacement commit. This was treated as a reproducibility defect rather than normalizing away the evidence.

```sh
Tools/Verify/Verify-Seed.sh
```

The Release build completed with zero warnings and errors. All 31 conformance tests passed, preserving every prior language, bytecode, runtime, WvDump, WVO, and Windvale-assembler case while adding complete Stage 0 linker coverage. The linker tests exercise actual-address alignment from aligned and unaligned bases, all section kinds, materialized BSS and padding, local/export/import resolution, both relocation kinds, semantic input order, locale independence, every WVL failure family reachable from external input, aggregate and map limits, relocation/address overflow, 200 deterministic hostile objects, full-image reconstruction, and exact image/map digests. The real CLI flow assembled both inputs, linked them, checked every essential map record and exact artifact digest, rejected an undefined import without output, preserved an existing image on rejection, surfaced a native missing-parent failure without output, and preserved every prior native-host case on both systems.

The Windows and Linux reports were then compared with the test runner:

```powershell
dotnet run --project Tests/Windvale.Seed.Tests/Windvale.Seed.Tests.csproj --configuration Release --no-build -- --compare-reports artifacts/seed-conformance-windows-x64.json artifacts/seed-conformance-debian-x86_64-9c4b9f5.json
```

The comparator confirmed equality between Microsoft Windows `10.0.26200` x64 and Debian GNU/Linux 12 x64 on .NET `10.0.10`. Both hosts produced:

- Module format `1.5`, object format `1.0`, assembly format `1`, and link format `1`.
- `Sum-Data.wvb`: `64134dfd779b353c5e501c9c23337a0c3849bfef2c97a63a07913705b0f10c6b`, result `29`.
- `Hello-Windvale.wvb`: `43d565c304cf2e2f5d886ee30b1fabf0b2fbfb0c8cd28bd932d85d5add0bf504`, output `Hello from Windvale` plus LF, result `0`.
- `Read-Wvb-Header.wvb`: `0cdf05f6c9e1fb1db0d5ab449207870b5e47cc248f187cd43cd9a5c3c9eee995`, result `1`.
- `Wv-Dump-Core.wvb`: `2957fc5523ae3ca16cf1aaeb9104c14a3342a0aefde9ac591bb689f744f1467f`, result `0`, with the complete hosted Sum report equal.
- `Wvo-Object-Core.wvb`: `a5d574ea646946b159d95bd7e51434bfcbf7545083a54541438a79a2e5e999df`, result `0`, output `Wrote WVO 1.0 bytes=189` plus LF.
- `Sample.wvo`: `006fd80183da7fbc71d3c6d63b65e6f3551765508fe9dba6f38ba80e002eb28a`; the directly retrieved Windows and Debian files were byte-for-byte equal.
- Assembled `Hello-Object.wvo`: `992c298a4f9b68dec27b7203a2770f2a37ef2016ea45e88d33ee21994060fe85`; the directly retrieved Windows and Debian CLI outputs were byte-for-byte equal.
- `Wva-Assembler-Core.wvb`: `7dbcf042f011adab5a04670973fc17b6b63d50fb08c09e8e54c3a4adb2c00825`, result `0`; the directly retrieved Windows and Debian modules were byte-for-byte equal.
- Hosted assembler object: `992c298a4f9b68dec27b7203a2770f2a37ef2016ea45e88d33ee21994060fe85`; the directly retrieved Windvale-written Windows and Debian objects were byte-for-byte equal to each other and to Stage 0.
- Hosted assembler report: `wvasm 1`, then `assembly status=valid object-bytes=218 sections=2 symbols=3 relocations=2 offset=403 line=22 column=1` on both hosts.
- `Console-Provider.wvo`: `486134e34bb32abadd233d1c3303acd9c313aa69d3874cafdce0fcb61b6e72ab`; the directly retrieved Windows and Debian objects were byte-for-byte equal.
- `Hello-Linked.bin`: 24 bytes, `0e02d447ec379e8bc8be373694d6ca14fdde0125550cbd34ee05b3ecc63ffe9a`; the directly retrieved Windows and Debian flat images were byte-for-byte equal.
- `Hello-Linked.wvmap`: 1,721 bytes, `31bc6a8e90d5f3049ae3e2eb0735a901923186d6a03ed40f22762b557b2ba5f4`; the directly retrieved Windows and Debian maps and the complete report-embedded maps were byte-for-byte equal.

This qualified roadmap gate 6F: the separate Windvale Linking 1 contract and C# Stage 0 flat-image oracle. At that point, the next Phase 6 gate was the Windvale-written verified-bytecode linker producing exact oracle images and maps through explicit hosted boundaries. The Windvale assembler remains qualified at `a689617`, semantic inspector at `cc57bf9`, lexical scanner at `e5fd109`, WVA 1 Stage 0 oracle at `3bfc6bb`, Phase 5 at `f87a5fa`, Phase 4 at `a829fc8`, Phase 3 at `1f4b48a`, the Phase 2 WVB 1.3 slice at `e6c51c6`, WVB 1.2 at `a4b0f5d`, and WVB 1.1 at `60fd261`; none is a compatibility promise or supported obsolete input format. Future bytecode, assembly, object, link, compiler, verifier, runtime, host-adapter, or report-contract changes must regenerate both native reports rather than inheriting this evidence automatically.

## Local Windvale-owned console-application verification evidence

[Decision 0132](../Decisions/0132-Windvale-Owned-Console-Application-Verification.md) moves completed PE/ELF acceptance into portable Windvale while retaining the existing detailed C# parsers as independent recovery oracles. One logical application is presented as a first value of at most 4 MiB and at most 8,304 trailing bytes. The verifier regenerates the canonical sparse construction recipe, checks every literal and implicit-zero byte, skips only its one opaque native span, and returns fixed `WVCV 1` evidence plus the exact recovered native image through a named write.

The portable core is 46,074 bytes with SHA-256 `326e2ecfc1f4dd8bd24f71fff4a9db960de2519d9aae17afbcd6a005c2e7c94d`; its retained hosted bridge is 46,150 bytes with SHA-256 `74542907a1b7a90d6d13ee157e7a9e7a4e60e83c042a5486e2f0ab3113ad6013`. The combined ownership test recompiles both source sets, checks exact embedded identity and only `file.read_bytes`/`file.write_bytes`, proves deterministic evidence, rejects malformed evidence and noncanonical chunks without output, and recovers exact 4 MiB native images from both maximum completed applications. The maximum Linux case uses the full 8,304-byte second chunk.

The existing PE and ELF malformed-input corpora now require rejection by both the portable and independent C# verifiers, avoiding a duplicate application corpus. Both live writers require target, native bytes, and entry to agree under both implementations. Canonical output is unchanged: 5,120-byte PE SHA-256 `5947c00a81f4cf94651d42d619f3173a622448d042f4fa20e3042940d4a56c77` and 8,304-byte ELF SHA-256 `8af8b46c290965cfc4475d882ac2d5fbdb0ffe4c493a19883a19c2683a319ec4`.

Windows Development completes in 90.6 seconds on the merged candidate with a zero-warning Release build, all 76 regular Seed tests in 74.315 seconds, and all 31 bounded OS tests. The combined verifier, Windows container, and Linux container cases take 484, 1,430, and 144 milliseconds. The affected combined case also passes independently in 1.320 seconds after the upstream replay.

Exact descendant `ea1aa89` passes GitHub [Verify run 30783457203](https://github.com/eworker-inc/Windvale/actions/runs/30783457203) on Windows and digest-pinned Debian 12. Both hosts pass all 77 Seed tests, all 31 OS tests, the golden compiler contract, and the native CLI gate with zero-warning Release builds. Windows Seed takes 241.987 seconds and Linux Seed 216.976 seconds; their golden cases take 169.303 and 150.484 seconds. This closes the fresh dual-host qualification requirement for the paired portable verifier and live Windows/Linux writers.

## Qualified standalone hosted console capability

[Decision 0156](../Decisions/0156-First-Standalone-Hosted-Console-Capability.md) adds `windows-x64-console-v2` and `linux-x64-console-v2` while retaining exact version-1 bytes. Each version-2 application binds the single required service `console.write_line` to explicit capability, service-table, platform-adapter, arena, output-leaf, native-image, and entry evidence in a 192-byte `WVHC 1` record. Canonical WVA startup code initializes ABI 22, context 7, service table 5, a 2 MiB record arena, and a 64 MiB dynamic-value arena. Windows imports exactly `GetStdHandle` and `WriteFile`; Linux uses direct `mmap`, `write`, and `exit` syscalls.

The independent PE/ELF verifier reconstructs untrusted container structure, WVA relocation targets, runtime tables, exact output leaf, imports or syscalls, padding, bounds, digests, native bytes, entry, and ordered service set. One paired corpus constructs both targets twice, verifies deterministic and cross-target native identity, and covers malformed metadata, rehashed leaf forgery, tables, imports, truncation, trailing data, and maximum sizes without cloning the earlier platform suites. On Windows, canonical `Hello-Windvale.wv` produces a 3,584-byte PE with SHA-256 `abb38c9de40d75eb8f4029aeb0d7f6dbba7b1ec776fefc8bc96aa376bd310f61`; the canonical Linux ELF is 9,216 bytes with SHA-256 `5103d02b883a40757281ccce027445cab59c2330833efeb60ec57d1a39363b78`.

Exact implementation commit `ed4a0b40b9f59b45f1aaedf9d147cac0330fd1cb` passes GitHub [Verify run 30802630910](https://github.com/eworker-inc/Windvale/actions/runs/30802630910). Windows and digest-pinned Debian 12 each complete a zero-warning Release build, all 86 Seed tests including the golden compiler contract, all 34 OS tests, and the native CLI gate. The paired test directly runs the current host's version-2 application, observes `Hello from Windvale` and result 0, and verifies both canonical containers. Windows Seed takes 364.810 seconds with a 205.826-second golden contract; Linux Seed takes 332.904 seconds with a 188.554-second golden contract. The complete verifier jobs finish in 12m03s and 10m39s. This qualifies the first serialized standalone host capability, exact PE/ELF packaging, and direct Windows/Linux execution. The separate 4 MiB WVO/link boundary and Stage 0 outer packagers remain the next Phase-10 work.

## Local exact-compiler native transport and service manifest

[Decision 0160](../Decisions/0160-Bounded-Large-Native-Object-And-Link-Admission.md) retains ordinary 4 MiB behavior and adds an explicit 20 MiB admission profile over unchanged WVO 1.0 and the shared flat linker. The exact compiler produces a deterministic 17,147,219-byte WVO with SHA-256 `ee1c77763ad7440ad87ec10c4b7def67f9ec296eb366277cfe219617c76dda4b`, restores its exact 17,130,441 native bytes, and emits canonical map SHA-256 `86e32c67a41ccb31053d4905191e32dd2aaafd59e4b73416ea2b401e83adc973`. Standard read, write, and link paths still reject it; only explicit `Largeˉnative` admission succeeds.

[Decision 0161](../Decisions/0161-Exact-Compiler-Service-Bundle-And-Manifest.md) measures the compiler's actual imports rather than binding all twelve ABI slots. Its exact six capabilities require ten services; `text.quote` and `i32.format` remain absent. Existing service generators and independent instruction verifiers produce one ordered platform bundle, and fixed 1,024-byte `WVHA 1` records bind capabilities, service-table slots, platform adapters, placements, sizes, flags, native identity, and each leaf identity.

Windows produces a 17,143,635-byte bundle / SHA-256 `6d524aa9b96d0f624b0b449937ec6c0987a57e2c002af8276784c63a185efef6` and manifest SHA-256 `b209eabbced72ccca37a325ac55f1a5198f9c257c6dc9faa5b57954c393c2493`. Linux produces a 17,143,351-byte bundle / `99da55911c81218ac74442a695d340ed440c74515b830bcc659bd4b7df7b2d4b` and manifest `46435a40a18f7a5462f256b829aea90032c0de2c18d415222e3d5133e81da507`. Both begin with the exact shared compiler native image. `WVHA 1` now serializes the audited 24,000,000,000-instruction compiler limit rather than leaving startup to inherit the ordinary one-million default; the module-boundary candidate fails at 20,000,000,000 and reproduces exact Stage 2 bytes at 24,000,000,000.

[Decision 0163](../Decisions/0163-Bounded-Hosted-Compiler-Runtime-Data.md) builds one platform-parameterized runtime plan rather than separate PE/ELF layouts. Both retain exact header, argument, snapshot, record, text, name, and file-data placements. Windows reserves 409,026,560 RW/NX bytes and produces initial-header SHA-256 `5d61f926461fc19e46e04a7e5dd3636fcbaa554e30370fc10a5eeb7992f5e634`; Linux reserves 406,929,408 bytes and produces `ee0e58ef5c82f65a48150f886ce7349753bb0af05145c46dafae000eff576c4a`. The difference is exactly the paired UTF-16 versus UTF-8 path scratch extents.

The post-rebase focused Windows Fast selection completes a zero-warning Release build and passes the expanded exact-compiler transport/runtime case in 9.019 seconds; total verifier wall time is 22.0 seconds. This deliberately reuses one compiler compilation and shared mutation loops instead of adding a duplicate suite. Cross-host Qualification, format-3 PE/ELF startup and outer verification, direct compiler execution, and .NET-free Stage 2 reproduction remain pending.

## Format-3 compiler application and candidate publication evidence

[Decisions 0164](../Decisions/0164-First-Exact-Compiler-Linux-Executable-Container.md) and [0167](../Decisions/0167-First-Exact-Compiler-Windows-Executable-Container.md) add canonical WVA startups plus separate format-3 builders and untrusted-input parsers. The exact Linux static-PIE ELF is 17,158,144 bytes / SHA-256 `42f3f947cccca8e44c279afce1b6e944682dc440e0e9cda6546883898d951f31`; it has no interpreter, dynamic table, imports, or loader relocations. [Decision 0168](../Decisions/0168-Direct-Packaged-Compiler-Stage2-Reproduction.md) runs each raw platform executable over the shared canonical twelve-source inventory. Each child returns zero, emits `source wvb status=Valid functions=328 code-bytes=481356 module-bytes=599868`, and produces the exact 599,868-byte compiler WVB / SHA-256 `9673bf3331763181f443ec67b7a513bc66daa718969f7f6b0d197a4186071066`. Windows module snapshots observe `KERNEL32.DLL` and `SHELL32.DLL` and no named CLR or .NET host/runtime module. Linux `/proc/<pid>/maps` snapshots observe the raw ELF mapping and no named .NET host/runtime mapping.

[Decision 0169](../Decisions/0169-Public-Format3-Compiler-Targets.md) exposes those independently verified containers as `windows-x64-console-v3` and `linux-x64-console-v3`. The public writers reproduce the pinned internal-builder bytes from the same one compiler fragment, and the CLI sends both through the existing unique-sibling, write-through, flushed, Linux-mode-prepared, atomic-replacement publisher. Atomic failure/replacement/mode evidence stays in the one shared publication case rather than being duplicated for each format.

The final focused Fast compiler selection on 2026-08-03 completes a zero-warning Release build and passes the expanded exact-compiler case in 21.779 seconds; total verifier wall time is 29.5 seconds. It includes deterministic WVO/link evidence, exact platform bundles and metadata, shared malformed-input checks, both public writers, complete outer-container verification, and the one direct Windows canonical Stage 2 run. Exact implementation commit `db20fefaa3333b7b78392ba12141d1ae2b6bb0c2` subsequently passes GitHub [Verify run 30816153900](https://github.com/eworker-inc/Windvale/actions/runs/30816153900). ## Qualified Probe-36 contained service-failure evidence

[Decision 0165](../Decisions/0165-Contained-Windvale-Service-Failure.md) retains ABI 22, context 7, WVA seam 11, `WVKMEM14`, paging 4, interpreter profile 7, and the Probe-35 resources while advancing the internal records to `WVPROC15` and `WVCHAN04`. A fifth firmware scenario completes the existing resource lookup, sends a 37-byte directory request whose declared total is 36, and makes init reject it through a CPL3 general-protection fault. The kernel accepts only the exact live service/waiter/counter/message shape, records init as the faulted peer, clears every transient channel field, increments close and wake counts once, resumes the waiting client with exact transport result `-1`, and revokes its resources after a clean three-syscall exit.

The zero-warning Windows Release build and all 38 focused OS tests pass. The process-policy WVB is 10,333 bytes / `32ee6cd53018a71dd7f2c2596f1a2242622a2f261539f70e164cd8b5d84ba43c`; its WVO is 70,050 / `2f71db723bbcbdedcf5d4f6512230e65537644d02fbda1709debeaf170af58ca`. ## Qualified Probe-37 kernel-owned service-endpoint evidence

[Decision 0172](../Decisions/0172-First-Kernel-Owned-Service-Endpoint.md) retains ABI 22, context 7, `WVKMEM14`, `WVCHAN04`, interpreter profile 7, the exact process extents and mappings, and all five boot scenarios while advancing the process record to `WVPROC16`. One new 64-byte kernel-only `WVENDP01` sits between process capability reference `65536` and the retained channel. It binds provider reference `65537`, current client reference `65538` or `131074`, channel identity, open/closed state, provider outcome, and bounded resolution/close counters.

Every capability-bearing syscall now resolves the process-local reference and right through the endpoint before touching channel or resource state. The normal path records eight successful resolutions in generation 1, requires that boundary before rebinding the endpoint to generation 2, records eight more, and closes once with provider-exited status. The contained-service-fault path records six resolutions and closes once with provider-faulted status. Independent lifecycle coverage rejects truncated records, stale clients, resolution after close, replayed close, invalid rebinds, and malformed provider states. The portable Windvale policy models endpoint identity, rebind, and both closure outcomes; Stage 0 retains explicit serialization and x86-64 transition seams.

The zero-warning Windows Release build and all 38 focused OS tests pass. ## Metered Windvale-native ABI-22 x86-64 selector and scalar calls

[Decisions 0190](../Decisions/0190-First-Windvale-Native-X64-Lowering-Slice.md), [0194](../Decisions/0194-Straight-Line-Windvale-Native-X64-Lowering.md), [0195](../Decisions/0195-Acyclic-Control-Flow-Windvale-Native-X64-Lowering.md), [0204](../Decisions/0204-Metered-Loops-In-Windvale-Native-X64-Lowering.md), [0205](../Decisions/0205-Bounded-Direct-Calls-In-Windvale-Native-X64-Lowering.md), and [0206](../Decisions/0206-Register-Scalar-Calls-In-Windvale-Native-X64-Lowering.md) transfer the current scalar selector rather than replaying Decision 0059's obsolete six-byte prototype. The portable Windvale core independently verifies capability-free WVB 1.6 with either one `Main() -> i32` or one `i32` helper with zero through four `i32`/`bool` parameters followed by parameterless `Main`. Both functions retain bounded scalar locals, checked arithmetic, comparisons, boolean negation, typed empty-stack blocks, forward and backward jumps/branches, loops, and early returns. `Main` may call helper index zero through the shared ABI-22 instruction and depth budgets; arguments use `R8D`, `R9D`, `ECX`, and `EDX` and become the helper's first ordinary local cells. The core algorithmically emits the same bytes and standard WVO as Stage 0: 406/479 bytes for the constant case, 1,871/1,944 for combined arithmetic, 4,835/4,908 for nested control, 1,665/1,738 for the metered loop, 795/902 for the parameterless call, and 2,581/2,688 for a four-parameter mixed-scalar call.

The mixed-scalar code and WVO SHA-256 identities are `1a0a541d2bd59378b4fa6df53248c3c359e909a0b7446198ebb1a58ca5a79721` and `cb7d2c74edb7aa3443e1e23cf0d762d4c15b79c39ea4f363531b2ec80633c13f`. The core, memory-adapter, and hosted-tool WVB identities are `75bc5ed88d2da94e602957ea9df6751470e277a135de76286c249d163983e26d`, `34acd0b6b0b58eb08c737c1e86b940314223a23ee968aabdfb098ec645462930`, and `bb76b15ba551f16101ecef600f47fabb8098e5c9b903a17aa14d893e9fe1854d`. The hosted tool lowers through Stage 0 to 1,114,491 code bytes and a 1,116,927-byte WVO. One existing conformance test still compiles the modules once. It compares the differential scalar/control/loop/call corpus against Stage 0, retains the loop's exact 157/156 instruction boundary and the parameterless call's exact instruction/depth boundaries, covers mixed types in all four scalar register positions, rejects stack-underflow, invalid-local, invalid-branch, unreachable-cycle, changed-call-target, and mismatched-parameter-type shapes, runs every retained oracle through the natively compiled tool, and preserves a pre-existing sentinel output on truncated WVB.

This is current-host implementation evidence, not cross-host qualification. It transfers one real metered multi-function machine-byte path with the complete register-only scalar argument convention, not general call graphs, stack-passed or descriptor parameters, recursion, data, capabilities, relocations, fragment verification, container construction, the complete backend, or the .NET retirement gate.

## Local initializer-inference and typed-constant candidate

The 2026-08-03 local compiler candidate implements nonparameter local initializer inference, uniform multiline trailing commas, and explicit typed constants in both Stage 0 and the Windvale-written frontend. Stage 0 accepts `i32`, `i64`, `u8`, `u32`, `u64`, `bool`, and enum constants. The current Windvale WVB 1.6 compiler accepts the executable subset `i32`, `u8`, `u32`, `bool`, and enum; `i64` and `u64` remain deferred until that compiler's WVB 1.7 value support. Constant evaluation is checked, exact-type, deterministic, limited to literals, enum members, earlier root constants, parentheses, and already implemented operators. Forward references, imported constants, invalid names/types/initializers, calls, mismatches, unsupported operators, and overflow reject before WVIR or WVB publication.

`WVLB 1.1` carries shape `0` only for an inferred nonparameter local, while typed lowering and the independent WVIR validator establish its exact first-store shape. `WVSD 1.1` adds constant declaration entries. Constant reads lower to existing typed literal or enum WVIR operations and produce no local, data, export, or runtime identity. The updated `Function-Only.wv` fixture still emits the exact 815-byte WVB module at SHA-256 `9ccfed0509e84bfc63979c6dc13170c14762efbdaa448b4c5894325f31aa7761`, demonstrating byte-for-byte storage-free lowering.

Focused Windows Fast tests pass for the Stage 0 constant evaluator and each Windvale compiler phase from lexer through canonical executable WVB. The editor grammar verifier also passes with `const` keyword and ALL_CAPS constant-name capture. At that constants-only checkpoint, local compiler artifacts included `Source-Symbols-Core.wvb` at 314,510 bytes / `ee9e083e075180964fb8111344073dbc6746d2eb9a0c7dd6e4024af0988ff82f`, `Source-Wir-Core.wvb` at 558,143 / `efb70649f19798c82e581b1c7677a5dae140093ec7972b2cdafc67d875d2afbb`, and `Source-Wvb-Core.wvb` at 642,993 / `47b4d8fe3fac69deeaaf930440b1f51933e00336f325f3fe69a452aa4252a5e9`. Those were local deterministic candidates only: the golden compiler contract, Stage 1/Stage 2 bootstrap, Linux execution, and dual-host Qualification had not been rerun and no successor cross-host claim was made.

The next 2026-08-03 local slice implements named record literals and block-form `else if` in Stage 0, the Windvale-written body parser, bindings, typed WVIR, WVB path, differential fixture, and editor grammar. Unknown record targets and unknown, duplicate, missing, or mismatched fields reject deterministically. Field expressions lower left to right in source order, then their temporaries are placed in field declaration order before the existing `Recordˉcreate` operation; the migrated 1,781-byte nominal fixture retains SHA-256 `1366b543a28a1921aca6198bca9eaaf5eeeb97766405d5efcdeff9d27cfca57a`. `else if` becomes recursive conditional blocks and introduces no new WVIR operation or WVB opcode.

Focused Windows Fast tests pass for Stage 0 named-record diagnostics/execution and the Windvale body, binding, WVIR, and executable-WVB phases, including source-set, graph, and symbol dependency artifacts. The editor verifier covers named-record type recognition without misclassifying an ordinary condition block. ## Local loop-control, short-circuit, and compound-assignment candidate

The next Decision 0184 slice implements nearest-loop `break` and `continue`, Boolean-only left-to-right short-circuit `&&` and `||`, and `+=`, `-=`, and `*=` in Stage 0 and the Windvale-written compiler. `break` and `continue` outside a loop reject as `WVC2111` and `WVC2112`. Compound assignment is restricted to a mutable local, reads its target once, applies the retained checked same-type arithmetic, and stores once. The WVB 1.6 Windvale path admits `i32` and `u32`; Stage 0 also follows its existing `i64` and `u64` arithmetic contract.

WVIR appends structurally verified `Boolˉphi = 64`. A phi operation emits no WVB opcode: its two unconditional predecessor jumps materialize the selected Boolean operand before the ordinary join. Validation requires the exact two-predecessor short-circuit shape and rejects conditional or additional predecessors. A differential control oracle combines nested precedence, an out-of-bounds right operand that must remain unexecuted, both loop exits, and all three compound assignments. Stage 0 and Windvale produce byte-identical verified WVB, and the reference runtime returns `7`.

## Consolidated 2026-08-03 language-evolution candidate

The current working tree supersedes the intermediate local checkpoints above with one coherent compiler/runtime candidate. Stage 0 and the Windvale-written path now agree on initializer inference, uniform trailing commas, storage-free typed constants across every scalar width, required import aliases with explicit export privacy, named record construction, block-form `else if`, application and capability metadata, exhaustive non-fallthrough `match`, nominal payload variants and explicit recoverable-result shapes, bounded immutable sequences, affine local builders, deterministic `for`, loop control, short-circuit Boolean flow, compound assignment, checked division/remainder, bitwise operations, shifts, exact text/bytes equality, and exact little-endian `u64` byte codecs. Both emit only WVB 1.11, verify the complete metadata/type/opcode contract before execution, and reject malformed indices, shapes, bounds, shifts, builder states, and arithmetic failures deterministically. Narrow native, WebAssembly, Foundation, and OS consumers validate named WVB 1.11 subsets rather than relying on older format versions.

The resource-lifetime portion remains intentionally at its accepted design boundary: no `using` syntax or typed provider/resource cleanup behavior is emitted until a named decision fixes the value shape, provider binding, cleanup order, failure interaction, and manifest representation. This is the only documented slice not converted into implementation; inventing those semantics would violate the decision-first boundary.

Exact Stage 0 and Windvale-produced feature fixture SHA-256 identities are `a64840ef59dbf72ee48acda5e430af3d3c52eb933a3647b72b23566c12bbaf6d` for match (result 2), `c6bab822805f36cf44952bae5b0d2f7d4818c8dea19c6d2ef803d93a7846e2d1` for the explicit fallthrough oracle (result 3), `55a19d814522c8183efe62840af5f1e15af544a607e90ac876cb9e00f0ea0cff` for metadata, `a86b891960c9e9d04f5f79689e4edab471e5b3002640b0d60c001fd53f0058c5` for variants (result 45), `c8a61eb6254b701c012b0b3c5fba47a485822e28976f684d973af0efffb236d3` for the 809-byte collection fixture (result 16), `26fcb52bc7e893d306d6f12343fa596bdbf919c95c74f033ef67c2afaf46a210` for the 2,623-byte Windvale operator subset (result 28), and `6d64f0d9ee597e655c18457a7ea6f40dd67a4cea2ad24b525782977739240b29` for the wide-scalar, explicit `u32` to `u64` widening, and `u64` codec fixture (result 64). ## Local checked u64 database-storage geometry candidate

Implemented-candidate [Decision 0211](../Decisions/0211-U64-Database-Storage-Geometry.md)
adds the lossless `U64ˉfromˉu32(u32) -> u64` Foundation intrinsic and canonical
WVB 1.11 opcode `0xBF`. Stage 0, the Windvale-written bindings/WVIR/WVB path,
the bytecode verifier and inspector, the reference runtime, and the portable
Windvale-written verifier agree on the new type effect. The narrow native,
WebAssembly, and OS execution subsets remain free to reject `u64` modules
until those named profiles deliberately widen.

`Libraries/Database/Storage-Geometry.wv` is a portable, capability-free checked
geometry layer. It widens the `u32` page size once, computes a `u64` page offset
and exclusive end only after division-based overflow preflights, and returns
typed `Invalidˉpageˉsize`, `Arithmeticˉoverflow`, or `Outsideˉstorage` failures.
Tests cover ordinary pages, offsets beyond 4 GiB, the maximum `u32` page
identifier, multiplication/addition/exclusive-end overflow, a zero page size,
storage bounds, and malformed request envelopes.

The final Windows checks complete zero-warning Release builds and pass the
four-test Fast database selection, both WVB 1.11 wide-scalar/codecs tests, the
Windvale compiler phase and exact-byte parity checks, the 93.959-second extended
exact-compiler native transport/package case, and the 510.737-second aggregate
golden cross-host contract. These are local Windows candidate results, not a
new dual-host qualification. The next database gate is a decision for the
rights-limited random-access storage resource contract; no durable page writer,
flush promise, single-writer lease, or recovery format is claimed yet.

## Local native WVB-to-WVO candidate evidence

[Decision 0224](../Decisions/0224-First-Native-Wvb-To-Wvo-Front-Door.md)
moves the existing hosted lowerer shell into `Compiler/Windvale` and packages it
as paired `WVHN 1` source candidates without new startup assembly. Direct
`Bytesˉfromˉi32ˉlittle` replaces the former text-formatting conversion used to
emit signed machine fields. The complete retained differential corpus proves that
this changes no generated WVO byte.

[Decision 0228](../Decisions/0228-Bounded-Acyclic-Native-Call-Directory.md)
expands the core from one optional helper to one through eight functions under a
strictly decreasing call-ordinal rule. A focused 16-byte-per-function directory
owns machine offsets, lengths, parameter counts, and four padded scalar types;
the separate layout module also owns WVO function-symbol encoding. The lowering
core falls from 2,979 to 2,887 source lines rather than growing another special
case.

[Decision 0231](../Decisions/0231-Native-I32-Static-Data-Lowering.md)
adds one canonical immutable `[i32]` data declaration, `data.length`, and
bounds-checked `data.load.i32`. The focused 99-line data module owns exact WVB
payload admission, the 154-line object module owns optional `.rodata`, data
symbols, and relative relocations, and the layout module contracts to 82 lines
of function-directory ownership. The instruction core remains the owner of typed
stack analysis, exact machine sizes, the `WVR3005` branch, and RIP-relative load
selection.

[Decision 0232](../Decisions/0232-General-Native-Call-Directory.md)
replaces the decreasing-ordinal and Main-last restrictions with three bounded
passes: complete signature admission, machine measurement, and emission. The
export identifies parameterless `Main` at any ordinal, while every in-range exact
scalar signature may be called forward, backward, recursively, or cyclically
under ABI 22's shared instruction and call-depth budgets. The focused object
writer keeps local helper symbols ordered before exported `Main`; layout adds the
previously unnecessary `$function_0007` name.

[Decision 0233](../Decisions/0233-Bounded-Native-U8-U32-Scalars.md)
adds the `u8`/`u32` locals, constants, checked `u32.add`, `u32.less`, `u8.equal`,
and typed scalar helper returns required by compiler-produced `Function-Only.wv`.
The packed 16-byte directory now records one-byte parameter count and return type,
two reserved bytes, and four parameter types. The layout module also owns typed
temporary-slot offsets in canonical `i32`, `bool`, `u8`, `u32` order. Unmeasured
scalar opcodes remain fail-closed.

The current module identities are:

- data: `9a32b3854270bcace52f615633f4b110d9f0777ba5fb5338157af0965dcc8ed4`;
- layout: `9213e44e9b3a4cfec1c35acc8ddb211e4d5ddc057f3033c0152d9dfa102f0ee6`;
- object: `430eb454207189b369c99ee622ca0f7b90edcc3b1be15ebaee52d73da95ce6c8`;
- core closure: `e3f418faa2669453972547e46b2b5a4d5d553d65445a9941e5de47fb8b59ce73`;
- memory adapter: `d052845962f3f5dbab7eac6acbb0b0fd3d84afaa24e4f81e2fb12e6b1814624d`;
- 112,091-byte hosted tool: `836151775e9f0cf3f92258f2271b13934c5cb4e2e89e3aad9cbbbdd1b9bf5e51`.

The tool's complete Stage 0 lowering records 1,392,878 native code bytes and a
1,396,532-byte WVO. Its Windows candidate is 1,411,072 bytes /
`6d12b981a87a5ab8ccd15df7a7679c79967d6509a888ae47545b5b8b2262c5f7`;
its Linux candidate is 1,413,120 bytes /
`088803a03d175e05d9482e8d184d47ae7f5301298f4fe073563c186c657a8ece`.

One stable accepted-subset fixture is 174 WVB bytes /
`7933c4ba0cb854477a95750966f9532c2b9eb5888e55ec9ae64ebdf552a08f31`.
The native Windows candidate emits its exact 479-byte WVO /
`0d1829bbbc77f3ee3910a70f98528e1078117480332adb5a2d09df8b2d25f3b5`
and reports 406 code bytes. The independent C# WVO parser admits that result.

Before execution, the shared-backend assertions retain the existing three-function
`Add -> Build -> Main` fixture and add canonical `Sum-Data.wv`. Its exact 493-byte
WVB returns 29 and now produces Stage 0's byte-identical 3,288-byte WVO through

bytes, `$data_0000`, and one `Relative_i32` relocation with addend `-4`. Every
retained code-only object remains byte-identical. An encoded data index one fails
closed as `Unsupportedˉcode`.

The general-call oracle begins with compiler-emitted order `Alpha, Main, Zeta`,
so `Main` contains a real forward edge. A verifier-approved same-signature
mutation connects the two recursive helpers in both directions. The reference
runtime and Stage 0 native path return 42 at call depth five and reach `WVR3004`
at four. The Windvale memory adapter, hosted tool, and generated native tool
produce Stage 0's exact 4,350-byte `.text` and 4,491-byte WVO. The retained
out-of-range call target continues to fail closed as `Unsupportedˉcode`.

Canonical `Function-Only.wv` returns 6 through `u32` loop state and checked
addition, a `u8` argument/comparison, and a Boolean helper result. Stage 0
interpretation and native execution agree on 6. The Windvale memory adapter,
hosted tool, and generated native tool reproduce Stage 0's exact 6,041-byte
`.text` and 6,216-byte WVO.

Review then identified that this canonical fixture does not exercise the admitted
`u8` and `u32` helper-result slots. A small source-defined vector adds

execution, and reproduces Stage 0's exact 2,349-byte `.text` and 2,490-byte WVO
through the hosted lowerer.

final source check. The qualified native build compiles the final 77-function

with the memory identity above. One focused shared-backend pass completed the
existing behavior before its deliberately last stale identity; those identities
were transcribed without replaying unchanged behavior. The subsequent test review
found the scalar-result gap above, so that newly affected shared selection ran once:
it builds in 7.74 seconds and passes its single case in 5.773 test seconds,
including the new vector and every retained differential assertion. The distinct
package selection builds in 8.36 seconds and completes direct Windows execution,

reported final Windows/Linux identities were transcribed without replaying that
unchanged package behavior. Standard, Qualification, the full Seed/OS suites,

cutover were deliberately not run.

This is focused local candidate evidence only. Standard, Qualification, the full

map, 406-byte flat image, and current-host version-1 console application.

The fixed link-map and flat-image SHA-256 identities are
`857710249807d2fed4da847729d0244f08ccdc70156c043fdaa0516de394e2dc`
and `7c05565142850adab1d63d999479977a23ef50c7264c03ee55ce5b323df26408`.
The 2,560-byte Windows PE identity is
`8f2c3389dafa40c0231a0f5aeead3db5570697d54874f324a81f84a2d5b16eb6`;
the future grouped Linux run must reproduce the recorded 8,304-byte ELF identity
`fe525b84b9bf902677a5c7beb36872dfd72e7d6d0f12bfb5c95d491c4e1cd3f7`.

On local Windows, the zero-warning compile-only Seed build completes in 20.46
seconds. The single focused composition case passes in 7.443 test seconds. It
parses each portable/container boundary, requires exported `Main` at offset zero,
recovers the exact linked bytes from the PE, executes it to process result 42,
and observes no named CLR/.NET mapping in the lowerer, linker, packager, or result
process. Standard, Qualification, the full Seed/OS suites, native CLI qualification,
Linux execution, GitHub verification, artifact promotion, and ordinary-path cutover
remain deferred. The final broad gate begins with an upstream refresh and one
reconciliation of the accumulated retirement state.

## Local expanded native portable-test evidence

[Decision 0226](../Decisions/0226-Expanded-Native-Portable-Test-Plan.md)
extends the fixed native inventory from two to five existing portable fixtures.
The 635-byte UTF-8/LF `WVNT 1` plan has SHA-256
`79294d8e1a08325cd41042e6068b4a6bc9f3c15bd05372ad4bb7eda268a47b73`.
The three added WVB identities are the existing independent compiler-conformance
values for `function-only`, `data-text`, and `nominal-types`; their source-defined
results are the already asserted `6`, `13`, and `11`.

After reviewing the plan digest in both host launchers, each pre-existing WVB
identity, the reference/interpreter outcomes, and the managed wrapper's expected
report, the direct .NET-free Windows plan passes all five cases in 2.236 seconds.
Each case builds through the qualified native source front door, requires exact
WVB bytes, and executes through the pinned native runner candidate. No managed
compiler, runtime, or test process participates in that run.

This is focused local candidate evidence only. The managed suite was not rebuilt
or rerun, because the affected wrapper contains only the reviewed fixed report and
the direct plan is the narrowest behavioral check. Linux execution, malformed and
failure-plan transfer, broader fixture orchestration, Standard, Qualification,
GitHub verification, and runner/test promotion remain deferred to the grouped
end-of-goal gate.

## Local native result-and-failure test evidence

[Decision 0227](../Decisions/0227-Native-Result-And-Failure-Test-Plan.md)
supersedes the unqualified five-result plan with the fixed eight-case `WVNT 2`
result/failure contract. Its 1,074 UTF-8/LF bytes have SHA-256
`619b22496a6999b80ed25f601066c1fa07162dad52c6b6c79b9d836a1d46df62`.
The three added WVB identities, failure statuses, and executed guest-instruction
counts exactly match the pre-existing independent scalar-interpreter assertions:
invalid UTF-8 is `3014:11`, byte-range rejection is `3008:14`, and truncated
little-endian `u16` input is `3016:4`.

Review found and corrected one stale version-1 header skip in the Linux adapter
before execution. The final review checks both launcher digests, the version-2
header, the closed result/failure policy, all three conformance identities and
oracles, and the managed wrapper's complete eight-case report. The direct
.NET-free Windows plan then passes all eight cases in 3.336 seconds. Each negative
case passes only with exit `1`, empty standard output, and its exact one-line native
diagnostic; a generic nonzero result is insufficient.

This focused run subsumes the preceding five-case local result. The managed suite,
Linux execution, malformed-module envelopes, additional runtime/resource failures,
Standard, Qualification, GitHub verification, and runner/test promotion remain
deferred to the grouped end-of-goal gate.

## Local native malformed-WVB test evidence

[Decision 0229](../Decisions/0229-Native-Malformed-Wvb-Test-Fixtures.md)
supersedes the unqualified result/failure plan with the fixed thirteen-case
`WVNT 3` contract. Its 1,983 UTF-8/LF bytes have SHA-256
`1b5dc525a2a5fc8883e21cbd0502bb2c3af1cb93c32fec11f5379e9f624fd870`.
The first eight rows and every prior WVB/result/failure identity remain unchanged.

Five checked-in base64 text fixtures decode to exact malformed WVB values derived
once from the existing 174-byte return-42 vector: bad magic (174 bytes,
`20618498d9df059d52fc0d660bf52f32df291c88b94d4b5ded224078f936108e`),
bad format version (174 bytes,
`4f0cc323d4eb6713405a3e92f7c885b358aa1efc5fa08e2ecca7be7e17287614`),
invalid module-name UTF-8 (174 bytes,
`d7e26806542fc5a924193f7d42a229b8e86aaab78c7ba9f845f0b51dcc655c55`),
one-byte truncation (173 bytes,
`c795b6a811dac439e60775dfc1c48b23b6a594e203639bc660f974d41dc4073f`),
and one appended zero byte (175 bytes,
`7121dfd48f36433738e81c9328e54b86ac3e26aa5a61c89f5bef6469df0481c1`).
The host adapters verify each decoded identity before invoking the already
qualified native verifier.

Review covered the closed two-input/three-expectation policy, both launcher
digests, all decoded hashes, exact exit/channel requirements, and the managed
wrapper's complete report before execution. The direct .NET-free Windows plan
passes all thirteen cases in 5.1 seconds. Every malformed case returns 1, writes
no standard output, and writes exactly
`wvb status=Invalid phase=semantic` plus LF to standard error.

The managed wrapper was not rebuilt or rerun because the direct plan is the
narrowest behavioral check and its fixed report was reviewed. Linux execution,
typed-execution and control-reachability corruptions, randomized malformed data,
unsafe bytecode, Standard, Qualification, GitHub verification, and runner/test
promotion remain deferred to the grouped end-of-goal gate or later focused
transfers.

## Local native typed/control malformed-WVB evidence

[Decision 0230](../Decisions/0230-Native-Typed-And-Control-Malformed-Fixtures.md)
supersedes the unqualified thirteen-case plan with the fixed twenty-two-case
`WVNT 4` contract. Its 3,906 UTF-8/LF bytes have SHA-256
`bb681b63e0dada74e2fc8cd0dd029a1ec17a0c341677d4f2bbcd9f568f5f022d`.
The first thirteen rows and every prior WVB/result/failure identity remain
unchanged.

Eight typed-execution fixtures derive from the exact native-built nominal-types
WVB, while the capability-argument fixture derives from the same hosted-capability
source used by the existing conformance case. Their documented byte changes mirror
the reviewed managed corruption definitions but are not interpreted at test time.
Each checked-in base64 value is decoded and bound by its complete SHA-256 before
the qualified native verifier runs. Eight require exact `typed-execution`
rejection; the changed jump target requires exact `control-reachability`
rejection.

Review confirms 22 unique six-field rows, all 14 decoded fixture identities, and
an exact match between plan order and the managed wrapper's expected PASS report.
The direct .NET-free Windows plan passes all 22 cases in 5.794 seconds. Every new
case returns 1, writes no standard output, and writes only its exact one-line native
phase diagnostic to standard error.

The managed wrapper was not rebuilt or rerun because its complete expected report
was reviewed and the direct plan is the narrowest behavioral check. Linux
execution, remaining malformed limits, randomized malformed inputs, the broader
unsafe-bytecode corpus, Standard, Qualification, GitHub verification, and
runner/test promotion remain deferred to the grouped end-of-goal gate or later
focused transfers.
